SYSTEM TEXT (verbatim; V1 uses it as is):
 You are the mind of a small home robot. A separate safety program drives the wheels, avoids
 obstacles and may refuse any action. Each time something happens, you choose the robot's next
 action, using the mission, what just happened and the memory notes.

 Actions:
 explore: drive on and explore the home
 approach: drive to a person
 greet: stop and say hello or goodbye to a person
 look: stop and look around in place
 wait: stay still and wait

 Rules (the first rule that fits decides):
 1. Someone asks the robot to stop, stay, wait, hold on or pause: wait.
 2. Someone asks the robot to come, come here or follow them: approach.
 3. Someone says hello or goodbye to the robot, or waves at it: greet.
 4. Someone asks the robot to look at or check something: look.
 5. Follow memory notes about people (for example: leave them alone, or greet them).
 6. Quiet mission: do not approach or greet a person who has not invited the robot; explore.
 7. A person is busy, does not want the robot, or tells it to carry on or go: explore.
 8. A person is talking with the robot: wait.
 9. The mission or task is finished: wait.
 10. The person the robot was approaching is gone: look.
 11. The robot entered a new room: look. A look-around is finished: explore.
 12. Mission about people: a far or medium person: approach; a close person not yet greeted: greet.
 13. Otherwise: keep the current activity.

EXAMPLES (V2 and V3 append these after the rules, verbatim, under "Examples:"):
 Mission: Explore and map the flat. | Current activity: exploring | You entered a new room: the laundry room. -> look
 Mission: Find people and keep them company. | Current activity: exploring | Someone said: "Wait for me here." -> wait
 Mission: Explore and map the flat. | Current activity: waiting | Someone said: "Follow me to the hallway." -> approach
 Mission: Find people and keep them company. | Current activity: looking | A person appeared far away in the hallway. -> approach
 Mission: Find people and keep them company. | Current activity: waiting | A person walked up to you and is now close. -> greet
 Mission: Map the flat quietly and do not disturb anyone. | Current activity: exploring | A person is sitting close, typing on a laptop. -> explore
 Mission: Map the flat quietly and do not disturb anyone. | Current activity: exploring | A person at medium distance said: "Hey robot, come here!" -> approach
 Mission: Find people and keep them company. | Current activity: approaching | A person close said: "Sorry, I have no time now." -> explore
 Mission: Help the owner find his keys. | Current activity: looking | The owner said: "Never mind, I found them." -> wait
 Mission: Explore and map the flat. | Current activity: exploring | Someone said: "What's under the table?" -> look
 Mission: Explore and map the flat. | Current activity: exploring | A person close said: "See you later, robot!" -> greet
 Mission: Find people and keep them company. | Current activity: looking | You finished looking around the garage. -> explore
 Mission: Explore and map the flat. | Current activity: exploring | Nothing new happened. -> explore

VARIANTS (all: Gemma 4 template via /apply-template, thinking off, system = text above, user = state
lines + tail, cache_prompt true, temperature 0):
 V1 rules only. Tail: "Which action next? Answer with one word: explore, approach, greet, look or wait."
 V2 rules + examples. Same tail as V1.
 V3 rules + examples + reason. Tail: "Which action next? First write one short sentence naming the
   rule that fits. Then write a new line starting with 'Action:' and one word: explore, approach,
   greet, look or wait." Step 1: generate, n_predict 48, stop at the first newline. Step 2: append
   "\nAction:" to the model turn and score the next token over the five names (check distinct first
   tokens in that position). Record the reason text.
 ORDER: FWD as above; REV reverses the five names in the tail (system text unchanged).
 Report system-prompt token counts; stop if V2/V3 system + longest item + 64 > 2048.

PROCEDURE (one setup_q4 launch, ctx 2048; blocks of 40 items, 60 s pause between blocks):
 DEV: V1-FWD, V1-REV, V2-FWD, V2-REV, V3-FWD, V3-REV.
 SELECTION (fixed now): among variants with DEV order flips <= 4, the highest mean acceptable
   accuracy over both orders; tie -> V1, then V2, then V3. If none has flips <= 4, the highest mean
   acceptable anyway, flagged FLIPS. Write selection.json (variant, DEV metrics), log its SHA-256.
 TEST: only the selected variant, FWD then REV. Official result.
 PASS (fixed now, on TEST): acceptable >= 0.80 in both orders AND strict >= 0.65 in both orders AND
   flips <= 4/40.
METRICS as B1 for every block (strict, acceptable, recall per label, confusion, copy rate, near-ties
 < 0.05, flips per variant), plus for V3 the reason texts.

FILES: benchmark/mind_picker/b2_rules/{b2.py, test_b2.py, README.md}; offline tests with a fake server
(three variants' rendering, two-step V3 scoring, selection rule incl. tie and FLIPS case, TEST lock
before selection, symlink custody fix). Run folder ~/robot_runs/mind_b2/<UTC>/ with run.json,
requests.jsonl, selection.json, report.txt, artifacts.sha256.
Known B0 limitation (interrupt/cleanup finalization) is out of scope; the Reviewer may note it as P3.

STEPS: implement -> offline tests -> show item 3 of TEST rendered for V3 (offline, before the run) ->
one phone run -> invoke the Reviewer automatically per WORKFLOW (source-only method if the Codex
sandbox cannot execute in proot; say so) -> freeze -> return the review verbatim, stop for my decision.

RETURN: report.txt verbatim; DEV table (variant x order: strict, acceptable, flips); selection;
TEST table and PASS/FAIL; 5 sample V3 reasons; review verbatim; git status --porcelain=v1 -uall.
Label results VALID / INVALID / NOT A MEASUREMENT. No commit or push.
END

   3  examples + one reason sentence first (V3). Research only: no motors, camera, YOLO, M2, main.py or run_mission.py; no

  18  Names must have distinct first tokens in the scored position: checked with /tokenize for every DEV prompt before any
  19  inference (V3 with an empty reason), again for each V3 item with its actual reason, and for the TEST prompts before
  20  TEST inference; a problem STOPs (INVALID).

  22  rule) -> selection.json, SHA-256 logged; TEST: the selected variant FWD then REV. PAUSE_S between blocks. Metrics per
  23  block are B1's (b1.metrics); PASS on TEST is B1's rule (acceptable >= 0.80 and strict >= 0.65 in both orders, flips
  24  <= 4). VALID only with all 320 rows without error, one server PID, exit 0 without SIGKILL, no exception or stop, and

 118  def inside_repo(path):
 119      p = Path(path).resolve()  # symlinks resolved (B1 review P1)
 120      return any(q.exists() and os.path.samefile(q, REPO) for q in [p, *p.parents])  # bind mounts: same inode
 121  
 122  
 123  def load_set(path, want, custody=True):
 124      """(set, bytes SHA-256): refused when custody and the path is inside the repo (checked before reading) or the
 125      hash differs. 'visit' in labels, current activity and allowed lists reads 'approach'."""
 126      if custody and inside_repo(path):
 127          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {path} is inside the repository (test-set custody)')
 128      data = Path(path).read_bytes()
 129      if want is not None and sha(data) != want:
 130          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {path} SHA-256 {sha(data)[:12]} != pinned {want[:12]}')
 131      s = json.loads(data)
 132      ren = {'visit': 'approach'}
 133      for it in s['items']:
 134          it['current'], it['strict'] = ren.get(it['current'], it['current']), ren.get(it['strict'], it['strict'])
 135          it['allowed'], it['also_ok'] = [ren.get(n, n) for n in it['allowed']], [ren.get(n, n) for n in it['also_ok']]
 136      return s, sha(data)
 137  
 138  
 139  def load_test(out, logged_sha, path):
 140      """The TEST set, only after selection.json exists with the logged SHA-256."""
 141      sel = out / 'selection.json'
 142      if not logged_sha or not sel.exists() or sha(sel.read_bytes()) != logged_sha:
 143          raise SystemExit('refused: TEST is locked until selection.json is written and its SHA-256 logged')
 144      return load_set(path, TEST_SHA256)
 145  
 146  
 147  def state(s, it):
 148      return '\n'.join([f'Mission: {s["missions"][it["mission"]]}', f'Current activity: {ACTIVITY[it["current"]]}',
 149                        f'What just happened: {it["event"]}', f'Memory: {it["memory"] or "none"}',
 150                        f'Allowed actions: {", ".join(it["allowed"])}'])
 151  
 152  
 153  def offered(it, order):
 154      return it['allowed'] if order == 'FWD' else it['allowed'][::-1]
 155  
 156  
 157  def tail(variant, names):
 158      words = f'{", ".join(names[:-1])} or {names[-1]}.'
 159      if variant == 'V3':
 160          return ('Which action next? First write one short sentence naming the rule that fits. Then write a new line '
 161                  f"starting with 'Action:' and one word: {words}")
 162      return f'Which action next? Answer with one word: {words}'
 163  
 164  
 165  def messages(s, it, variant, order):
 166      return [{'role': 'system', 'content': SYSTEM[variant]},
 167              {'role': 'user', 'content': state(s, it) + '\n\n' + tail(variant, offered(it, order))}]
 168  
 169  
 170  def first_ids(post, text, ids, names, sep=''):
 171      """({name: first token id after text}, problems): text's ids must stay a prefix; first tokens distinct."""
 172      out, why = {}, []
 173      for n in names:
 174          full = b0.tokenize(post, text + sep + n)
 175          if full[:len(ids)] != ids or len(full) == len(ids):
 176              why.append(f'{n!r}: prompt ids change or no token follows')
 177          else:
 178              out[n] = full[len(ids)]
 179      if len(set(out.values())) != len(out):
 180          why.append(f'first tokens not distinct: {out}')
 181      return out, why
 182  
 183  
 184  def preflight(post, s, blocks, info, tag):
 185      """prompts[(variant, order, id)] = (text, ids, {name: id}); every prompt rendered and tokenized, no /completion.
 186      Problems go to info['problems']."""
 187      prompts, sizes = {}, {}
 188      for v, order in blocks:
 189          for it in s['items']:
 190              names = offered(it, order)
 191              text, ids, bad = b0.render(post, messages(s, it, v, order))
 192              if v == 'V3':  # scored position checked with an empty reason; rechecked per item with the real one
 193                  t2 = text + ACTION
 194                  nid, why = first_ids(post, t2, b0.tokenize(post, t2), names, ' ')
 195              else:
 196                  nid, why = first_ids(post, text, ids, names)
 197              info['problems'] += [f'{tag} {v}-{order} item {it["id"]}: {w}' for w in why + ([bad] if bad else [])]
 198              prompts[v, order, it['id']] = (text, ids, nid)
 199              sizes.setdefault(v, []).append(len(ids))
 200              info.setdefault('name_ids', {}).setdefault('V3' if v == 'V3' else 'V1/V2', {}).update(nid)
 201              if tag == 'DEV' and it['id'] == SHOW_ITEM and order == 'FWD':
 202                  info[f'prompt_{v}_dev_item{SHOW_ITEM}'] = post('/detokenize', {'tokens': ids})['content']
 203      for v in sizes:
 204          info.setdefault('system_tokens', {})[v] = len(b0.tokenize(post, SYSTEM[v], special=False))
 205          info.setdefault('prompt_tokens', {})[f'{tag} {v}'] = (min(sizes[v]), max(sizes[v]))
 206          if max(sizes[v]) + CTX_MARGIN > CTX:
 207              info['problems'].append(f'{tag} {v}: longest prompt {max(sizes[v])} + {CTX_MARGIN} > {CTX}')
 208      return prompts
 209  
 210  
 211  def score(post, it, v, order, prompt):
 212      """One row: probabilities over the offered names (raw and renormalised), top, margin, counters; V3 adds the
 213      reason and its step-1 counters."""
 214      names, (text, ids, nid) = offered(it, order), prompt
 215      t0, row = time.perf_counter(), {'variant': v, 'order': order, 'id': it['id'], 'offered': names}
 216      if v == 'V3':
 217          r1 = post('/completion', {'prompt': ids, **b0.DECODE, **REASON, 'cache_prompt': True})
 218          reason = r1['content']
 219          t2 = text + reason + ACTION
 220          ids = b0.tokenize(post, t2)
 221          nid, why = first_ids(post, t2, ids, names, ' ')
 222          if why:
 223              raise RuntimeError(f'scored position after the reason: {why}')
 224          tm1 = r1.get('timings', {})
 225          row.update(reason=reason, reason_tokens=r1.get('tokens_predicted'), reason_stop=r1.get('stop_type'),
 226                     reason_prompt_n=tm1.get('prompt_n'), reason_cache_n=tm1.get('cache_n'))
 227      body = {'prompt': ids, **b0.DECODE, **b0.PICK, 'cache_prompt': True}
 228      res = post('/completion', body)
 229      raw, norm = b0.probs(res, {n: [nid[n]] for n in names})
 230      if norm is None:
 231          raise RuntimeError(f'item {it["id"]}: an offered name is outside the top {b0.PICK["n_probs"]}: {raw}')
 232      top = max(norm, key=norm.get)  # ties: first offered
 233      second = sorted(norm.values())[-2] if len(norm) > 1 else 0.0
 234      tm = res.get('timings', {})
 235      row.update(prompt_tokens=len(ids), prompt_ids_sha256=sha(json.dumps(ids)), prompt_n=tm.get('prompt_n'),
 236                 cache_n=tm.get('cache_n'), prompt_ms=tm.get('prompt_ms'), wall_s=round(time.perf_counter() - t0, 3),
 237                 probs_raw=raw, probs=norm, top=top, margin=norm[top] - second)
 238      return row
 239  
 240  
 241  def block_metrics(rows, s, v):
 242      """B1's metrics for one variant: (FWD block, REV block, {'flips', 'verdict'})."""
 243      # ponytail: reuses b1.metrics by relabelling the variant as B1's arm A; own metrics if B1's layout ever changes
 244      m = b1.metrics([{**r, 'arm': 'A'} for r in rows if r.get('variant') == v and r.get('set') == s['tag']], s)
 245      return m['blocks']['A-FWD'], m['blocks']['A-REV'], m['arms']['A']
 246  
 247  
 248  def select(dev):
 249      """Fixed rule. dev[v] = (FWD, REV, arm) from block_metrics. Among variants with flips <= MAX_FLIPS the highest
 250      mean acceptable over both orders; tie -> V1, then V2, then V3. None qualifies: highest mean anyway, flag FLIPS."""
 251      mean = {v: (dev[v][0]['acceptable'] + dev[v][1]['acceptable']) / 2 for v in VARIANTS}
 252      ok = [v for v in VARIANTS if len(dev[v][2]['flips']) <= MAX_FLIPS]
 253      pool = ok or VARIANTS
 254      best = max(pool, key=lambda v: (mean[v], -VARIANTS.index(v)))
 255      return {'variant': best, 'flag': None if ok else 'FLIPS', 'rule': select.__doc__.split('. ', 1)[1],
 256              'dev': {v: {'FWD': {k: dev[v][0][k] for k in ('strict', 'acceptable')},
 257                          'REV': {k: dev[v][1][k] for k in ('strict', 'acceptable')},
 258                          'mean_acceptable': mean[v], 'flips': dev[v][2]['flips']} for v in VARIANTS}}
 259  
 260  
 261  def run_blocks(post, s, blocks, prompts, pid, emit, rows, info, first):
 262      """Score the blocks in order; False at the first request error (recorded, the run stops)."""
 263      for k, (v, order) in enumerate(blocks):
 264          if k or not first:
 265              time.sleep(PAUSE_S)
 266          t0 = time.monotonic()
 267          for it in s['items']:
 268              try:
 269                  row = score(post, it, v, order, prompts[v, order, it['id']])
 270              except Exception as e:  # fail closed: recorded, the run ends
 271                  row = {'variant': v, 'order': order, 'id': it['id'], 'error': f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'}
 272              row['set'], row['pid'], row['pid_start'] = s['tag'], pid, b0.pid_start(pid)
 273              rows.append(row)
 274              emit(row)
 275              if 'error' in row:
 276                  info['stopped'] = f'{s["tag"]} {v}-{order} item {it["id"]} failed: {row["error"]}'
 277                  return False
 278          dur = round(time.monotonic() - t0, 1)
 279          info.setdefault('blocks', []).append({'block': f'{s["tag"]} {v}-{order}', 'duration_s': dur,
 280                                                'over_warn': dur > BLOCK_WARN_S})
 281      return True
 282  
 283  
 284  def run_all(post, dev, test_path, out, pid, emit):
 285      """DEV preflight and blocks, selection.json, then TEST (only now opened) for the selected variant.
 286      Returns (rows, info); info['test'] = TEST set and hash when it was opened."""
 287      rows, info = [], {'problems': []}
 288      try:
 289          dev['tag'] = 'DEV'
 290          prompts = preflight(post, dev, DEV_BLOCKS, info, 'DEV')
 291          n_ctx = post('/props', None).get('default_generation_settings', {}).get('n_ctx')
 292          info['n_ctx'] = n_ctx
 293          if n_ctx != CTX:
 294              info['problems'].append(f'server n_ctx {n_ctx!r} != {CTX}')
 295          if info['problems']:
 296              info['stopped'] = 'STOP before any inference: ' + '; '.join(info['problems'])
 297              return rows, info
 298          if not run_blocks(post, dev, DEV_BLOCKS, prompts, pid, emit, rows, info, True):
 299              return rows, info
 300          sel = select({v: block_metrics(rows, dev, v) for v in VARIANTS})
 301          (out / 'selection.json').write_text(json.dumps(sel, indent=1) + '\n')
 302          info['selection'], info['selection_sha256'] = sel, sha((out / 'selection.json').read_bytes())
 303          emit({'event': 'selection', 'variant': sel['variant'], 'flag': sel['flag'],
 304                'selection_sha256': info['selection_sha256']})
 305          test, info['test_sha256'] = load_test(out, info['selection_sha256'], test_path)
 306          test['tag'], info['test'] = 'TEST', test
 307          blocks = [(sel['variant'], 'FWD'), (sel['variant'], 'REV')]
 308          prompts = preflight(post, test, blocks, info, 'TEST')
 309          if info['problems']:
 310              info['stopped'] = 'STOP before TEST inference: ' + '; '.join(info['problems'])
 311              return rows, info
 312          run_blocks(post, test, blocks, prompts, pid, emit, rows, info, False)
 313      except Exception as e:
 314          info['exception'] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 315          info['stopped'] = f'exception after {len(rows)} requests: {info["exception"]}'
 316      return rows, info
 317  
 318  
 319  def validity(rows, info, run):
 320      why = []
 321      n = len(DEV_BLOCKS) * run['dev_rows'] + 2 * run.get('test_rows', 40)
 322      if len(rows) != n or any('error' in r for r in rows):
 323          why.append(f'{len(rows)}/{n} rows, {sum("error" in r for r in rows)} errors')
 324      if len({(r['pid'], r['pid_start']) for r in rows}) > 1:
 325          why.append('more than one server PID')
 326      if run.get('exit_code') != 0 or run.get('killed'):
 327          why.append(f'server exit {run.get("exit_code")}, SIGKILL {run.get("killed")}')
 328      for k in ('exception', 'stopped'):
 329          if info.get(k) or run.get(k):
 330              why.append(f'{k}: {info.get(k) or run.get(k)}')
 331      for k in ('dev', 'test'):
 332          if run.get(f'{k}_sha256_end') != run.get(f'{k}_sha256'):
 333              why.append(f'{k} SHA-256 at the end {run.get(f"{k}_sha256_end")} != start {run.get(f"{k}_sha256")}')
 334      return ('VALID' if not why else 'INVALID'), why
 335  
 336  
 337  def table(L, title, rows, s, variants):
 338      L += ['', title, 'block      strict        acceptable    copy rate      near-ties (margin < 0.05)']
 339      res = {}
 340      for v in variants:
 341          F, R, arm = res[v] = block_metrics(rows, s, v)
 342          for o, x in (('FWD', F), ('REV', R)):
 343              L.append(f'{v}-{o:<7} {x["strict"]:.3f} {round(x["strict"] * x["n"]):>2}/{x["n"]}  '
 344                       f'{x["acceptable"]:.3f} {round(x["acceptable"] * x["n"]):>2}/{x["n"]}  '
 345                       f'{(x["copy_rate"] or 0):.3f} {x["copy"][0]:>2}/{x["copy"][1]}  {x["near_ties"]}')
 346      L.append('recall per strict label (hits/n):')
 347      L += [f'  {v}-{o:<4} ' + '  '.join(f'{a} {h}/{n}' for a, (h, n) in x['recall'].items())
 348            for v in variants for o, x in zip(('FWD', 'REV'), res[v][:2])]
 349      L.append('confusion (rows strict, columns chosen):')
 350      L += [f'  {v}-{o:<4} ' + '; '.join(f'{a} -> ' + ', '.join(f'{c} {n}' for c, n in sorted(r.items()))
 351                                         for a, r in sorted(x['confusion'].items()))
 352            for v in variants for o, x in zip(('FWD', 'REV'), res[v][:2])]
 353      L.append('copied current activity (ids): ' + '; '.join(f'{v}-{o} {x["copied_ids"]}' for v in variants
 354                                                             for o, x in zip(('FWD', 'REV'), res[v][:2])))
 355      L += [f'flips {v}: {len(res[v][2]["flips"])} {res[v][2]["flips"]}' for v in variants]
 356      by = {(r['variant'], r['order'], r['id']): r for r in rows if r.get('set') == s['tag']}
 357      L.append('id  strict (also ok)       ' + ''.join(f'{v}-{o:<12}' for v in variants for o in ('FWD', 'REV')))
 358      for it in s['items']:
 359          cells = []
 360          for v in variants:
 361              for o in ('FWD', 'REV'):
 362                  r = by.get((v, o, it['id']), {})
 363                  cells.append(f'{r["top"]} {r["probs"][r["top"]]:.2f}' if 'top' in r else r.get('error', '-')[:14])
 364          lab = it['strict'] + (f' ({",".join(it["also_ok"])})' if it['also_ok'] else '')
 365          L.append(f'{it["id"]:<3} {lab:<22} ' + ''.join(f'{c:<15}' for c in cells))
 366      reasons = [r for r in rows if r.get('set') == s['tag'] and 'reason' in r]
 367      if reasons:
 368          L.append('V3 reasons (id order: top | stop | reason):')
 369          L += [f'  {r["order"]} {r["id"]:<3} {r.get("top", "-"):<8} {r.get("reason_stop")} | {r["reason"]!r}'
 370                for r in reasons]
 371      return res
 372  
 373  
 374  def report(run, rows, info):
 375      s_label, why = run['validity']
 376      L = [f'MIND-B2 {run["utc"]}  code {run["code_sha256"][:12]}  base {run["git_head"]}',
 377           f'DEV {run["dev_sha256"][:12]} ({run["dev_rows"]} rows, {run["dev_path"]}); TEST '
 378           f'{(run.get("test_sha256") or "not opened")[:12]} ({run["test_path"]})',
 379           f'server: {" ".join(run["command"])}',
 380           f'n_ctx {info.get("n_ctx")}; system tokens {info.get("system_tokens")}; prompt tokens (min, max) '
 381           f'{info.get("prompt_tokens")}',
 382           f'name first-token ids {info.get("name_ids")}',
 383           f'ACCURACY {s_label}' + (f': {"; ".join(why)}' if why else ' (temperature 0; 320/320 rows; one server PID; '
 384                                    'exit 0; set hashes unchanged)'),
 385           '* timings NOT MEASUREMENTS (agent resident allowed)']
 386      L += [f'block {x["block"]}: {x["duration_s"]} s*' + ('  OVER ~4 min (continued)' if x['over_warn'] else '')
 387            for x in info.get('blocks', [])]
 388      dev = {'tag': 'DEV', 'items': run['dev_items']}
 389      if any(r.get('set') == 'DEV' for r in rows):
 390          table(L, '=== DEV ===', rows, dev, VARIANTS)
 391      if sel := info.get('selection'):
 392          L += ['', f'SELECTION ({sel["rule"]}): {sel["variant"]}' + (f'  FLAG {sel["flag"]}' if sel['flag'] else '')
 393                + f'; selection.json SHA-256 {info["selection_sha256"]}']
 394          L += [f'  {v}: mean acceptable {d["mean_acceptable"]:.3f}, flips {len(d["flips"])}'
 395                for v, d in sel['dev'].items()]
 396      if info.get('test'):
 397          t = info['test']
 398          res = table(L, f'=== TEST (official; {sel["variant"]}) ===', rows, t, [sel['variant']])
 399          F, R, arm = res[sel['variant']]
 400          verdict = arm['verdict'] if s_label == 'VALID' else 'NO RESULT (run INVALID)'
 401          L += ['', f'PASS rule: acceptable >= {b1.PASS["acceptable"]} and strict >= {b1.PASS["strict"]} in both orders, '
 402                    f'flips <= {b1.PASS["max_flips"]}/40',
 403                f'  TEST {sel["variant"]}: strict {F["strict"]:.3f}/{R["strict"]:.3f}, acceptable '
 404                f'{F["acceptable"]:.3f}/{R["acceptable"]:.3f}, flips {len(arm["flips"])} {arm["flips"]} -> {verdict}']
 405      if f'prompt_V3_dev_item{SHOW_ITEM}' in info:
 406          L += ['', f'--- rendered prompt, V3 FWD, DEV item {SHOW_ITEM} (/detokenize) ---',
 407                info[f'prompt_V3_dev_item{SHOW_ITEM}']]
 408      if info.get('stopped'):
 409          L.append(f'STOPPED: {info["stopped"]}')
 410      if run.get('exception'):
 411          L.append(f'EXCEPTION (runner): {run["exception"]}')
 412      L.append(f'server exit code {run.get("exit_code")}; SIGKILL needed {run.get("killed")}')
 413      return '\n'.join(L) + '\n'
 414  
 415  
 416  def finish(out, run, rows, info):
 417      """Validity, run.json, report.txt and artifacts.sha256, always (also after an exception)."""
 418      run['validity'] = validity(rows, info, run)
 419      (out / 'run.json').write_text(json.dumps({**run, 'info': {k: v for k, v in info.items() if k != 'test'}},
 420                                               indent=1) + '\n')
 421      text = report(run, rows, info)
 422      (out / 'report.txt').write_text(text)
 423      (out / 'artifacts.sha256').write_text(''.join(f'{sha(p.read_bytes())}  {p.name}\n' for p in sorted(out.iterdir())
 424                                                    if p.name != 'artifacts.sha256'))
 425      return text
 426  
 427  
 428  def main():
 429      ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
 430      ap.add_argument('--test', default=str(TEST))
 431      a = ap.parse_args()
 432      if inside_repo(a.test):  # custody: refused before anything runs; the file itself is opened only after selection
 433          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {a.test} is inside the repository (test-set custody)')
 434      dev, dev_sha = load_set(DEV, DEV_SHA256, custody=False)
 435      if bad := mem1.pinned_problems()[0]:
 436          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {bad}')
 437      if not b0.repro.port_free():
 438          raise SystemExit('refused: port 8080 in use (never stops a server it did not start)')
 439      utc = time.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ', time.gmtime())
 440      out = OUT_ROOT / utc
 441      out.mkdir(parents=True)
 442      run = {'utc': utc, 'dev_path': str(DEV), 'dev_sha256': dev_sha, 'dev_rows': len(dev['items']),
 443             'test_path': a.test, 'test_sha256_pinned': TEST_SHA256, 'code_sha256': sha(Path(__file__).read_bytes()),
 444             'b1_sha256': sha(Path(b1.__file__).read_bytes()), 'b0_sha256': sha(Path(b0.__file__).read_bytes()),
 445             'git_head': subprocess.run(['git', '-C', str(HERE), 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], capture_output=True,
 446                                        text=True).stdout.strip(),
 447             'command': mem1.command(CTX), 'decode': {**b0.DECODE, **b0.PICK, 'cache_prompt': True}, 'reason': REASON,
 448             'system': SYSTEM, 'dev_items': dev['items'], 'timings': 'NOT MEASUREMENTS'}
 449      (out / 'run.json').write_text(json.dumps(run, indent=1) + '\n')
 450      with open(out / 'requests.jsonl', 'x') as rq, open(out / 'server.log', 'x') as log:
 451          def emit(row):
 452              rq.write(json.dumps(row) + '\n')
 453              rq.flush()
 454          killed, rows, info, proc, exc = False, [], {}, None, None
 455          try:
 456              proc = subprocess.Popen(mem1.command(CTX), stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
 457                                      start_new_session=True)
 458              t0 = time.monotonic()
 459              while not cr.healthy():
 460                  if proc.poll() is not None or time.monotonic() - t0 > b1.LOAD_TIMEOUT_S:
 461                      raise RuntimeError(f'llama-server not healthy (exit {proc.poll()})')
 462                  time.sleep(0.1)
 463              run['server_pid'] = proc.pid
 464              rows, info = run_all(b1.Server(), dev, a.test, out, proc.pid, emit)
 465          except BaseException as e:  # recorded; the report and manifest are still written, then a stop re-raises
 466              exc = e
 467              run['exception'] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 468          finally:
 469              if proc is not None and proc.poll() is None:
 470                  os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
 471                  try:
 472                      proc.wait(timeout=b1.TERM_WAIT_S)
 473                  except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
 474                      os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
 475                      proc.wait()
 476                      killed = True
 477              run.update(exit_code=proc.returncode if proc else None, killed=killed,
 478                         dev_sha256_end=sha(DEV.read_bytes()))
 479              if info.get('test'):
 480                  run.update(test_sha256=info['test_sha256'], test_rows=len(info['test']['items']),
 481                             test_items=info['test']['items'], test_sha256_end=sha(Path(a.test).read_bytes()))
 482      print(finish(out, run, rows, info) + f'\n{out}')
 483      if exc is not None and not isinstance(exc, Exception):
 484          raise exc
 485  
 486  
 487  if __name__ == '__main__':
 488      main()
END
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b2_rules/test_b2.py (221 lines)
   1  """Offline tests for b2.py with a fake server: rendering of the three variants, two-step V3 scoring, the selection rule
   2  (tie and FLIPS), the TEST lock, symlink custody. No server, no phone. Synthetic sets (the real TEST stays outside the
   3  repo)."""
   4  import json
   5  import os
   6  import re
   7  import sys
   8  import tempfile
   9  import unittest
  10  from pathlib import Path
  11  
  12  sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
  13  sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
  14  import b2  # noqa: E402
  15  import test_b0  # noqa: E402  B0's word-level fake server (b0_cache is on the path via b1)
  16  
  17  N = b2.NAMES
  18  MISSIONS = {'M1': 'Explore and map the flat.', 'M2': 'Find people and keep them company.'}
  19  DEV = {'missions': MISSIONS, 'items': [
  20      {'id': 1, 'mission': 'M1', 'current': 'explore', 'event': 'Someone said: "Stay right there."', 'memory': None,
  21       'allowed': ['explore', 'visit', 'greet', 'look', 'wait'], 'strict': 'wait', 'also_ok': []},
  22      {'id': 2, 'mission': 'M2', 'current': 'visit', 'event': 'A person is close.', 'memory': None,
  23       'allowed': ['explore', 'visit', 'look', 'wait'], 'strict': 'visit', 'also_ok': ['wait']},
  24      {'id': 3, 'mission': 'M2', 'current': 'explore', 'event': 'A person is lying on the couch, close.',
  25       'memory': 'Leo sleeps on the couch; do not wake him.', 'allowed': ['explore', 'visit', 'greet', 'look', 'wait'],
  26       'strict': 'explore', 'also_ok': ['look']}]}
  27  TEST = {'missions': MISSIONS, 'items': [
  28      {'id': 1, 'mission': 'M1', 'current': 'look', 'event': 'You finished looking around the hallway.', 'memory': None,
  29       'allowed': N, 'strict': 'explore', 'also_ok': []},
  30      {'id': 2, 'mission': 'M1', 'current': 'explore', 'event': 'Someone said: "Robot, over here!"', 'memory': None,
  31       'allowed': N, 'strict': 'approach', 'also_ok': []}]}
  32  
  33  
  34  class Fake(test_b0.Fake):
  35      """Tokens keep their leading space (' explore'); /completion answers a reason (n_predict 48) or the favourite
  36      action chosen by `pick(user_text, offered)`; names not offered get the highest raw probability."""
  37  
  38      def __init__(self, pick, alias=None):
  39          super().__init__()
  40          self.pick, self.alias, self.bodies = pick, alias or {}, []
  41  
  42      def ids(self, text):
  43          return [self.vocab.setdefault(self.alias.get(w, w), len(self.vocab) + 10)
  44                  for w in re.findall(r'<\|turn>|<turn\|>| ?\w+|\s|[^\w\s]', text)]
  45  
  46      def text(self, ids):
  47          inv = {v: k for k, v in self.vocab.items()}
  48          return ''.join(inv.get(i, '') for i in ids)
  49  
  50      def __call__(self, path, body=None):
  51          if path == '/health':
  52              return {'status': 'ok'}
  53          if path == '/detokenize':
  54              return {'content': self.text(body['tokens'])}
  55          if path != '/completion':
  56              return super().__call__(path, body)
  57          self.bodies.append(body)
  58          text = self.text(body['prompt'])
  59          if body['n_predict'] == 48:
  60              return {'content': 'Rule 1 fits', 'tokens_predicted': 3, 'stop_type': 'word',
  61                      'timings': {'prompt_n': 5, 'cache_n': 0}}
  62          offered = re.search(r'one word: (.*)\.<turn\|>', text).group(1).replace(' or ', ', ').split(', ')
  63          fav = self.pick(text.split('<|turn>user')[1], offered)
  64          sp = ' ' if text.endswith('Action:') else ''
  65          p = {self.vocab.setdefault(sp + k, len(self.vocab) + 10): 0.5 if k not in offered else 0.4 if k == fav else 0.05
  66               for k in N}
  67          return {'content': fav, 'timings': {'prompt_n': 3, 'cache_n': len(body['prompt']) - 3},
  68                  'completion_probabilities': [{'top_logprobs': [{'id': i, 'prob': v} for i, v in p.items()]}]}
  69  
  70  
  71  def by_label(user, offered):  # the strict label of the item whose event is in the user turn
  72      items = DEV['items'] + TEST['items']
  73      lab = next(it['strict'] for it in items if it['event'] in user)
  74      return {'visit': 'approach'}.get(lab, lab)
  75  
  76  
  77  def first(user, offered):
  78      return offered[0]
  79  
  80  
  81  def sets():
  82      d = json.loads(json.dumps(DEV))
  83      for it in d['items']:  # load_set's renaming, without a file
  84          for k in ('current', 'strict'):
  85              it[k] = {'visit': 'approach'}.get(it[k], it[k])
  86          it['allowed'] = [{'visit': 'approach'}.get(n, n) for n in it['allowed']]
  87          it['also_ok'] = [{'visit': 'approach'}.get(n, n) for n in it['also_ok']]
  88      return d
  89  
  90  
  91  def run(pick, alias=None):
  92      b2.PAUSE_S = 0
  93      fake, events = Fake(pick, alias), []
  94      d = tempfile.mkdtemp()
  95      tp = Path(d) / 'test.json'
  96      tp.write_text(json.dumps(TEST))
  97      b2.TEST_SHA256 = b2.sha(tp.read_bytes())
  98      rows, info = b2.run_all(b2.b1.Server(fake), sets(), tp, Path(d), 7, events.append)
  99      return fake, rows, info, events, Path(d)
 100  
 101  
 102  class T(unittest.TestCase):
 103      def test_rendering(self):
 104          s = sets()
 105          self.assertEqual(s['items'][1]['allowed'], ['explore', 'approach', 'look', 'wait'])
 106          st = b2.state(s, s['items'][1])
 107          self.assertEqual(st, 'Mission: Find people and keep them company.\nCurrent activity: approaching\n'
 108                               'What just happened: A person is close.\nMemory: none\n'
 109                               'Allowed actions: explore, approach, look, wait')
 110          m1, m2, m3 = (b2.messages(s, s['items'][1], v, 'REV') for v in b2.VARIANTS)
 111          self.assertEqual(m1[0]['content'], b2.SYS_RULES)
 112          self.assertTrue(b2.SYS_RULES.endswith('13. Otherwise: keep the current activity.'))
 113          self.assertEqual(m2[0]['content'], b2.SYS_RULES + '\n\nExamples:\n' + '\n'.join(b2.EXAMPLES))
 114          self.assertEqual((len(b2.EXAMPLES), m3[0]), (13, m2[0]))
 115          self.assertEqual(m1[1]['content'], st + '\n\nWhich action next? Answer with one word: wait, look, approach '
 116                                                  'or explore.')
 117          self.assertEqual(m2[1], m1[1])
 118          self.assertEqual(m3[1]['content'], st + "\n\nWhich action next? First write one short sentence naming the rule"
 119                           " that fits. Then write a new line starting with 'Action:' and one word: wait, look, "
 120                           "approach or explore.")
 121          fwd = b2.messages(s, s['items'][0], 'V1', 'FWD')[1]['content']
 122          self.assertTrue(fwd.endswith('one word: explore, approach, greet, look or wait.'))
 123  
 124      def test_v3_two_steps(self):
 125          fake, rows, info, _, _ = run(by_label)
 126          self.assertNotIn('stopped', info)
 127          v3 = [r for r in rows if r['variant'] == 'V3' and r['set'] == 'DEV']
 128          self.assertEqual(len(v3), 6)
 129          k = rows.index(v3[0])
 130          r1, r2 = fake.bodies[12], fake.bodies[13]  # V1 and V2 DEV blocks: 12 single requests, then V3 pairs
 131          self.assertEqual((r1['n_predict'], r1['stop'], r1['cache_prompt']), (48, ['\n'], True))
 132          self.assertEqual(r2['n_predict'], 1)
 133          self.assertEqual(fake.text(r2['prompt']), fake.text(r1['prompt']) + 'Rule 1 fits\nAction:')
 134          self.assertEqual(rows[k]['reason'], 'Rule 1 fits')
 135          self.assertEqual(info['name_ids']['V3'], {n: fake.vocab[' ' + n] for n in N})
 136          self.assertEqual(info['name_ids']['V1/V2'], {n: fake.vocab[n] for n in N})
 137          for r in rows:
 138              it = next(it for it in (DEV if r['set'] == 'DEV' else TEST)['items'] if it['id'] == r['id'])
 139              self.assertAlmostEqual(sum(r['probs'].values()), 1.0)
 140              self.assertEqual((list(r['probs']), r['top']), (r['offered'], by_label(it['event'], None)))
 141  
 142      def test_full_run_and_report(self):
 143          fake, rows, info, events, d = run(by_label)
 144          self.assertEqual(len(rows), 6 * 3 + 2 * 2)
 145          sel = json.loads((d / 'selection.json').read_text())
 146          self.assertEqual((sel['variant'], sel['flag']), ('V1', None))  # all 1.0: tie -> V1
 147          k = next(i for i, e in enumerate(events) if e.get('event') == 'selection')
 148          self.assertTrue(all(e['set'] == 'DEV' for e in events[:k]) and all(e['set'] == 'TEST' for e in events[k + 1:]))
 149          self.assertEqual({(e['variant'], e['order']) for e in events[k + 1:]}, {('V1', 'FWD'), ('V1', 'REV')})
 150          run_rec = {'utc': 'T', 'code_sha256': 'c' * 64, 'git_head': 'h', 'dev_path': '/d', 'dev_sha256': 'd' * 64,
 151                     'dev_sha256_end': 'd' * 64, 'dev_rows': 3, 'dev_items': sets()['items'], 'test_path': '/t',
 152                     'test_sha256': 't' * 64, 'test_sha256_end': 't' * 64, 'test_rows': 2, 'command': ['x'],
 153                     'exit_code': 0, 'killed': False}
 154          text = b2.finish(d, dict(run_rec), rows, info)
 155          self.assertIn('ACCURACY VALID', text)
 156          self.assertIn('-> PASS', text)
 157          self.assertIn("'Rule 1 fits'", text)
 158          text = b2.finish(d, {**run_rec, 'test_sha256_end': 'z'}, rows[:-1], info)
 159          self.assertIn('ACCURACY INVALID: 21/22 rows', text)
 160          self.assertIn('NO RESULT', text)
 161  
 162      def test_selection(self):
 163          def dev(acc, flips):  # acc[v] = (FWD acceptable, REV acceptable)
 164              return {v: ({'strict': 0, 'acceptable': acc[v][0]}, {'strict': 0, 'acceptable': acc[v][1]},
 165                          {'flips': list(range(flips[v]))}) for v in b2.VARIANTS}
 166          sel = b2.select(dev({'V1': (.5, .6), 'V2': (.7, .7), 'V3': (.9, .9)}, {'V1': 0, 'V2': 4, 'V3': 5}))
 167          self.assertEqual((sel['variant'], sel['flag']), ('V2', None))  # V3 excluded by flips
 168          sel = b2.select(dev({'V1': (.5, .6), 'V2': (.6, .5), 'V3': (.55, .55)}, {'V1': 1, 'V2': 0, 'V3': 0}))
 169          self.assertEqual(sel['variant'], 'V1')  # three-way tie on the mean -> V1
 170          sel = b2.select(dev({'V1': (.5, .6), 'V2': (.7, .7), 'V3': (.7, .7)}, {'V1': 9, 'V2': 0, 'V3': 0}))
 171          self.assertEqual(sel['variant'], 'V2')  # tie V2/V3 -> V2
 172          sel = b2.select(dev({'V1': (.5, .6), 'V2': (.7, .7), 'V3': (.8, .7)}, {'V1': 5, 'V2': 6, 'V3': 9}))
 173          self.assertEqual((sel['variant'], sel['flag']), ('V3', 'FLIPS'))
 174          self.assertEqual(sel['dev']['V3']['mean_acceptable'], .75)
 175  
 176      def test_flips_in_run(self):
 177          _, rows, info, _, _ = run(first)  # FWD -> first catalogue name, REV -> wait: every variant flips 3/3
 178          self.assertEqual(info['selection']['flag'], None)  # 3 <= 4
 179          self.assertEqual(info['selection']['dev']['V1']['flips'], [1, 2, 3])
 180  
 181      def test_test_lock(self):
 182          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 183              d, tp = Path(d), Path(d) / 'test.json'
 184              tp.write_text(json.dumps(TEST))
 185              b2.TEST_SHA256 = b2.sha(tp.read_bytes())
 186              with self.assertRaisesRegex(SystemExit, 'locked'):
 187                  b2.load_test(d, None, tp)
 188              (d / 'selection.json').write_text('{}')
 189              with self.assertRaisesRegex(SystemExit, 'locked'):
 190                  b2.load_test(d, 'f' * 64, tp)  # logged hash differs
 191              self.assertEqual(b2.load_test(d, b2.sha(b'{}'), tp)[0], TEST)
 192              b2.TEST_SHA256 = '0' * 64
 193              with self.assertRaisesRegex(SystemExit, 'pinned'):
 194                  b2.load_test(d, b2.sha(b'{}'), tp)
 195  
 196      def test_dev_stop_never_opens_test(self):
 197          fake, rows, info, events, d = run(by_label, alias={'look': 'wait'})  # look and wait share a first token
 198          self.assertIn('STOP before any inference', info['stopped'])
 199          self.assertEqual((rows, fake.bodies, events, 'test' in info), ([], [], [], False))
 200          self.assertFalse((d / 'selection.json').exists())
 201  
 202      def test_custody_symlink(self):
 203          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 204              link, dlink = Path(d) / 'set.json', Path(d) / 'repo'
 205              os.symlink(b2.DEV, link)  # outside symlink to a file inside the repo
 206              os.symlink(b2.REPO / 'benchmark', dlink)  # outside symlink to a directory inside the repo
 207              self.assertTrue(b2.inside_repo(link))
 208              self.assertTrue(b2.inside_repo(dlink / 'nonexistent.json'))
 209              with self.assertRaisesRegex(SystemExit, 'inside the repository'):
 210                  b2.load_set(link, None)
 211              real = Path(d) / 'real.json'
 212              real.write_text('{"items": []}')
 213              self.assertFalse(b2.inside_repo(real))
 214          self.assertTrue(b2.inside_repo(b2.HERE / 'x.json'))
 215          bind = Path('/termux-home/robot')  # the same repo through the proot bind mount: inode check
 216          if bind.exists():
 217              self.assertTrue(b2.inside_repo(bind / 'benchmark' / 'x.json'))
