BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b2_rules/README.md (28 lines)
   1  # MIND-B2 — picker quality: rules, worked examples, one reason sentence
   2  
   3  Research only (#146, after MIND-B1). With the policy written as explicit rules, does Gemma 4 E2B pick the owner's
   4  action on new situations? V1 rules only, V2 rules + 13 worked examples, V3 rules + examples + one reason sentence
   5  before the action; each in catalogue order (FWD) and reversed names (REV). Accuracy is deterministic (temperature 0;
   6  agent resident allowed); timings are recorded but are NOT MEASUREMENTS.
   7  
   8  - Actions: explore, approach (B1's visit renamed), greet, look, wait.
   9  - DEV: `../b1_quality/set/b1_set.json` (SHA-256 `b1abba5d…`, unchanged; "visit" read as "approach").
  10  - TEST: 40 owner-labelled items kept OUTSIDE the repo per WORKFLOW test-set custody:
  11    `/termux-home/mind_b2_test/b2_test.json`, SHA-256
  12    `d5faed98fb424ce8ca959ae58fe6f0ca1cd16d85bb7ed30f7cff8f74cd9f7b1f`, 40 rows, recorded 2026-11-02T14:29:08Z before
  13    any Gemma call. `b2.py` pins this hash, refuses a TEST path inside the repo (symlinks resolved, inode check kept
  14    for bind mounts; B1 review P1) and opens TEST only after `selection.json` is written and its SHA-256 logged.
  15  - Selection (fixed before the first Gemma call): among variants with DEV flips ≤ 4, the highest mean acceptable over
  16    both orders; tie → V1, V2, V3; none → the highest anyway, flagged FLIPS. PASS on TEST: acceptable ≥ 0.80 and
  17    strict ≥ 0.65 in both orders, flips ≤ 4/40 (B1's rule).
  18  - Imports B1 (`metrics`, `Server`) and B0 (`render`, `tokenize`, `probs`) unchanged. Rendering, V3's two steps and
  19    the validity conditions: see the docstring of `b2.py`.
  20  - Choices fixed before the first Gemma call: the system text's wrapped lines are joined into single lines (paragraphs
  21    and list lines kept); examples follow the rules after a blank line and `Examples:`; for an item with restricted
  22    allowed actions (DEV item 4) the tail names only the allowed ones (as B1); V3 scores the names after a space
  23    (`Action: wait` tokenizes as `Action`, `:`, ` wait`).
  24  - Run: `python b2.py` with Termux python (works from proot Debian); writes `~/robot_runs/mind_b2/<UTC>/`
  25    (run.json, requests.jsonl, selection.json, server.log, report.txt, artifacts.sha256).
  26  - Offline tests: `python -B test_b2.py` (fake server, synthetic sets; no phone).
  27  - Known limitation carried over from B0: an interrupt or a server-stop error can skip report.txt and
  28    artifacts.sha256 (completed rows stay in requests.jsonl).
END

## E. Context (unchanged, numbered with original line numbers)
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b1_quality/b1.py (379 lines)
   1  #!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/python
   2  """MIND-B1: picker decision quality, old layout (A) vs new layout (B), each in two option orders. Research only: no
   3  motors, camera, YOLO, M2, main.py or run_mission.py; no MTP, no projector. Accuracy is deterministic (temperature 0);
   4  timings are recorded but are NOT MEASUREMENTS (an agent may be resident).
   5  
   6  Set: 40 owner-labelled items kept OUTSIDE the repo (WORKFLOW test-set custody) at ~/mind_b1_set/b1_set.json, SHA-256
   7  pinned below (recorded before any Gemma call); a set path inside the repo is refused before it is read. Imports B0
   8  (b0_cache/b0.py, b0_set.json SHA-256 checked) and picker.py unchanged.
   9  State (both arms, same facts): Mission, Current activity (neutral gerund, never an option phrase), What just happened,
  10  Memory (or none), Allowed actions (catalogue order). Python removed the forbidden actions: only allowed ones are offered
  11  and scored, renormalised over them.
  12    A  picker.Gemma exactly: raw <state> block, letters, 'Answer: (', no chat template, cache_prompt false; options =
  13       the allowed b0_set.json catalogue lines ('<name>: <description>'); five letters A-E.
  14    B  B0 layout: Gemma 4 template (b0.render, checked against /apply-template, thinking off); system = SYS + catalogue
  15       (as b0.transcript, without mission/memory/events); user = state, blank line, B0 tail with the allowed names;
  16       first-token scoring over the names (b0.name_ids, b0.probs); cache_prompt true (shared system prefix); cache_n kept.
  17    FWD = catalogue order; REV: A reverses the options, B the names in the tail. Ties: first offered (as picker).
  18  One server launch (mem1.command(2048) = setup_q4 flags, pins checked), preflight (all 160 prompts rendered and
  19  checked, no /completion), then blocks A-FWD, A-REV, B-FWD, B-REV (40 items each, set order), PAUSE_S between blocks;
  20  a block over BLOCK_WARN_S is noted and the run continues. The first request error stops the run (INVALID).
  21  Metrics (metrics()) and the fixed PASS rule (PASS): see the task; the result is VALID only when all 160 rows exist
  22  without error, one server PID, exit code 0 without SIGKILL, no exception, and the set hash is unchanged at the end.
  23  
  24  Usage: b1.py [--set PATH]   (Termux python; from proot Debian or native Termux)
  25  Writes ~/robot_runs/mind_b1/<UTC>/: run.json, requests.jsonl, server.log, report.txt, artifacts.sha256.
  26  """
  27  import argparse
  28  import json
  29  import os
  30  import signal
  31  import subprocess
  32  import sys
  33  import time
  34  from pathlib import Path
  35  
  36  sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
  37  HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
  38  REPO = HERE.parents[2]
  39  sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE.parent / 'b0_cache'))
  40  sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO))
  41  import b0  # noqa: E402  render(), name_ids(), probs(), DECODE, PICK; imports mem1
  42  import picker  # noqa: E402  Gemma (layout A), LETTERS, http()
  43  
  44  mem1, cr, sha = b0.mem1, b0.cr, b0.sha
  45  SET = Path(cr.HOME) / 'mind_b1_set' / 'b1_set.json'
  46  SET_SHA256 = 'b1abba5daa3cb87c30a225af40d3e19e6f0feaf82ba95af48f8b48c4a23461eb'
  47  B0_SET_SHA256 = 'dddb288ace1642b1e93a6e268cb68ff1a028121b6853dfa837ac7849cfaa187b'
  48  OUT_ROOT = Path(cr.HOME) / 'robot_runs' / 'mind_b1'
  49  CTX = 2048
  50  ACTIVITY = {'explore': 'exploring', 'visit': 'visiting', 'greet': 'greeting', 'look': 'looking', 'wait': 'waiting'}
  51  BLOCKS = [('A', 'FWD'), ('A', 'REV'), ('B', 'FWD'), ('B', 'REV')]
  52  PAUSE_S, BLOCK_WARN_S = 60, 240
  53  NEAR_TIE = 0.05
  54  PASS = {'acceptable': 0.80, 'strict': 0.65, 'max_flips': 4}
  55  SHOW_ITEM = 11
  56  REQUEST_TIMEOUT_S, LOAD_TIMEOUT_S, TERM_WAIT_S = 300, 120, 30
  57  
  58  
  59  def inside_repo(path):
  60      p = Path(path).absolute()
  61      return any(q.exists() and os.path.samefile(q, REPO) for q in [p, *p.parents])  # bind mounts: same inode
  62  
  63  
  64  def load_set(path, want=SET_SHA256):
  65      """(set, bytes SHA-256): refused when the path is inside the repo (checked before reading) or the hash differs."""
  66      if inside_repo(path):
  67          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {path} is inside the repository (test-set custody)')
  68      data = Path(path).read_bytes()
  69      if want is not None and sha(data) != want:
  70          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {path} SHA-256 {sha(data)[:12]} != pinned {want[:12]}')
  71      return json.loads(data), sha(data)
  72  
  73  
  74  def state(s, it):
  75      return '\n'.join([f'Mission: {s["missions"][it["mission"]]}', f'Current activity: {ACTIVITY[it["current"]]}',
  76                        f'What just happened: {it["event"]}', f'Memory: {it["memory"] or "none"}',
  77                        f'Allowed actions: {", ".join(it["allowed"])}'])
  78  
  79  
  80  def offered(it, order):
  81      return it['allowed'] if order == 'FWD' else it['allowed'][::-1]
  82  
  83  
  84  def tail(names):  # B0's tail for the given names (equal to b0_set.json's tail for all five)
  85      return f'Which action next? Answer with one word: {", ".join(names[:-1])} or {names[-1]}.'
  86  
  87  
  88  def options(b, names):  # layout A: the catalogue lines of the offered names, in their order
  89      line = {c.split(':')[0]: c for c in b['catalogue']}
  90      return [line[n] for n in names]
  91  
  92  
  93  def messages(s, b, it, order):  # layout B
  94      return [{'role': 'system', 'content': '\n'.join([b['sys'], '', 'Actions:', *b['catalogue']])},
  95              {'role': 'user', 'content': state(s, it) + '\n\n' + tail(offered(it, order))}]
  96  
  97  
  98  class Server:
  99      """POST with B0's timeout, GET for body None (picker.http); keeps the last /completion body and response."""
 100  
 101      def __init__(self, post=None):
 102          self.post = post or (lambda path, body: picker.http(path) if body is None else
 103                               cr.post(path, body, timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT_S))
 104          self.last = None
 105  
 106      def __call__(self, path, body=None):
 107          res = self.post(path, body)
 108          if path == '/completion':
 109              self.last = (body, res)
 110          return res
 111  
 112  
 113  def preflight(post, s, b):
 114      """(gemma, prompts, info): every prompt of every block rendered, tokenized and checked; no /completion.
 115      prompts[(arm, order, id)] = ids (A) or (ids, {name: [id]}) (B). info['problems'] non-empty = STOP."""
 116      info = {'problems': []}
 117      g = picker.Gemma(post)
 118      letters = picker.LETTERS[:len(b['names'])]
 119      g.letter_ids = [g.encode(L)[0] for L in letters]  # picker builds four (its MODES); five actions here
 120      info['letter_ids'] = dict(zip(letters, g.letter_ids))
 121      if any(len(g.encode(L)) != 1 for L in letters) or len(set(g.letter_ids)) != len(letters):
 122          info['problems'].append(f'letters not single distinct tokens: {info["letter_ids"]}')
 123      prompts, sizes = {}, {'A': [], 'B': []}
 124      for arm, order in BLOCKS:
 125          for it in s['items']:
 126              names = offered(it, order)
 127              if arm == 'A':
 128                  ids = g.prompt_ids(state(s, it), options(b, names))
 129                  prompts[arm, order, it['id']] = ids
 130              else:
 131                  text, ids, bad = b0.render(post, messages(s, b, it, order))
 132                  name_ids, why = b0.name_ids(post, text, ids, names)
 133                  info['problems'] += [f'B {order} item {it["id"]}: {w}' for w in why + ([bad] if bad else [])]
 134                  prompts[arm, order, it['id']] = (ids, name_ids)
 135                  info.setdefault('name_ids', {}).update(name_ids)
 136              sizes[arm].append(len(ids))
 137              if it['id'] == SHOW_ITEM and order == 'FWD':
 138                  info[f'prompt_{arm}_item{SHOW_ITEM}'] = post('/detokenize', {'tokens': ids})['content']
 139      info['prompt_tokens'] = {a: (min(v), max(v)) for a, v in sizes.items()}
 140      info['n_ctx'] = post('/props', None).get('default_generation_settings', {}).get('n_ctx')
 141      if info['n_ctx'] != CTX:
 142          info['problems'].append(f'server n_ctx {info["n_ctx"]!r} != {CTX}')
 143      if max(max(v) for v in sizes.values()) >= CTX:
 144          info['problems'].append(f'a prompt does not fit ctx {CTX}: {info["prompt_tokens"]}')
 145      return g, prompts, info
 146  
 147  
 148  def score(post, g, s, b, it, arm, order, prompt):
 149      """One row: probabilities over the offered names (raw and renormalised), top, margin, counters."""
 150      names = offered(it, order)
 151      t0 = time.perf_counter()
 152      if arm == 'A':
 153          dist = g.decide(state(s, it), options(b, names))  # picker's own request (cache_prompt false)
 154          body, res = post.last
 155          if body['prompt'] != prompt:
 156              raise RuntimeError('picker.Gemma sent ids differing from the preflight')
 157          raw, norm = b0.probs(res, {n: [g.letter_ids[j]] for j, n in enumerate(names)})
 158          if max(abs(norm[n] - dist[c]) for n, c in zip(names, options(b, names))) > 1e-9:
 159              raise RuntimeError('letter probabilities differ from picker.Gemma.decide')
 160      else:
 161          ids, name_ids = prompt
 162          body = {'prompt': ids, **b0.DECODE, **b0.PICK, 'cache_prompt': True}
 163          res = post('/completion', body)
 164          raw, norm = b0.probs(res, {n: name_ids[n] for n in names})
 165          if norm is None:
 166              raise RuntimeError(f'item {it["id"]}: an offered name is outside the top {b0.PICK["n_probs"]}: {raw}')
 167      top = max(norm, key=norm.get)  # ties: first offered
 168      second = sorted(norm.values())[-2] if len(norm) > 1 else 0.0
 169      tm = res.get('timings', {})
 170      return {'arm': arm, 'order': order, 'id': it['id'], 'offered': names, 'prompt_tokens': len(body['prompt']),
 171              'prompt_ids_sha256': sha(json.dumps(body['prompt'])), 'cache_prompt': body['cache_prompt'],
 172              'prompt_n': tm.get('prompt_n'), 'cache_n': tm.get('cache_n'), 'prompt_ms': tm.get('prompt_ms'),
 173              'wall_s': round(time.perf_counter() - t0, 3), 'probs_raw': raw, 'probs': norm, 'top': top,
 174              'margin': norm[top] - second}
 175  
 176  
 177  def run_blocks(post, s, b, pid, emit):
 178      """Preflight, then the four blocks; returns (rows, info). Stops at the first problem or request error; an
 179      exception is recorded in info['exception'] and the rows collected so far are kept."""
 180      rows, info = [], {}
 181      try:
 182          g, prompts, info = preflight(post, s, b)
 183          if info['problems']:
 184              info['stopped'] = 'STOP before any inference: ' + '; '.join(info['problems'])
 185              return rows, info
 186          info['blocks'] = []
 187          for k, (arm, order) in enumerate(BLOCKS):
 188              if k:
 189                  time.sleep(PAUSE_S)
 190              t0 = time.monotonic()
 191              for it in s['items']:
 192                  try:
 193                      row = score(post, g, s, b, it, arm, order, prompts[arm, order, it['id']])
 194                  except Exception as e:  # fail closed: recorded, the run ends
 195                      row = {'arm': arm, 'order': order, 'id': it['id'], 'error': f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'}
 196                  row['pid'], row['pid_start'] = pid, b0.pid_start(pid)
 197                  rows.append(row)
 198                  emit(row)
 199                  if 'error' in row:
 200                      info['stopped'] = f'{arm}-{order} item {it["id"]} failed: {row["error"]}'
 201                      return rows, info
 202              dur = round(time.monotonic() - t0, 1)
 203              info['blocks'].append({'block': f'{arm}-{order}', 'duration_s': dur, 'over_warn': dur > BLOCK_WARN_S})
 204      except Exception as e:
 205          info['exception'] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 206          info['stopped'] = f'exception after {len(rows)} requests: {info["exception"]}'
 207      return rows, info
 208  
 209  
 210  def metrics(rows, s):
 211      """{'blocks': {'A-FWD': {...}}, 'arms': {'A': {...}}} from the recorded rows alone; denominators are the set's
 212      items (a missing row counts as wrong)."""
 213      items, n = {it['id']: it for it in s['items']}, len(s['items'])
 214      top = {(r['arm'], r['order'], r['id']): r['top'] for r in rows if 'top' in r}
 215      margin = {(r['arm'], r['order'], r['id']): r['margin'] for r in rows if 'top' in r}
 216      blocks, arms = {}, {}
 217      for arm, order in BLOCKS:
 218          got = {i: top.get((arm, order, i)) for i in items}
 219          strict = sum(got[i] == it['strict'] for i, it in items.items())
 220          ok = sum(got[i] in [it['strict'], *it['also_ok']] for i, it in items.items())
 221          recall, confusion = {}, {}
 222          for i, it in items.items():
 223              h, m = recall.get(it['strict'], (0, 0))
 224              recall[it['strict']] = (h + (got[i] == it['strict']), m + 1)
 225              row = confusion.setdefault(it['strict'], {})
 226              row[str(got[i])] = row.get(str(got[i]), 0) + 1
 227          copy_ids = [i for i, it in items.items() if it['strict'] != it['current']]
 228          copied = [i for i in copy_ids if got[i] == items[i]['current']]
 229          blocks[f'{arm}-{order}'] = {
 230              'n': n, 'rows': sum(v is not None for v in got.values()), 'strict': strict / n, 'acceptable': ok / n,
 231              'recall': {k: list(v) for k, v in sorted(recall.items())}, 'confusion': confusion,
 232              'copy_rate': len(copied) / len(copy_ids) if copy_ids else None, 'copy': [len(copied), len(copy_ids)],
 233              'copied_ids': copied,
 234              'near_ties': [i for i in items if margin.get((arm, order, i), 1.0) < NEAR_TIE]}
 235      for arm in ('A', 'B'):
 236          flips = [i for i in items if top.get((arm, 'FWD', i)) != top.get((arm, 'REV', i))]
 237          F, R = blocks[f'{arm}-FWD'], blocks[f'{arm}-REV']
 238          ok = min(F['acceptable'], R['acceptable']) >= PASS['acceptable'] and \
 239              min(F['strict'], R['strict']) >= PASS['strict'] and len(flips) <= PASS['max_flips']
 240          arms[arm] = {'flips': flips, 'verdict': 'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}
 241      return {'blocks': blocks, 'arms': arms}
 242  
 243  
 244  def validity(rows, info, run):
 245      why = []
 246      if len(rows) != len(BLOCKS) * run['set_rows'] or any('error' in r for r in rows):
 247          why.append(f'{len(rows)} rows, {sum("error" in r for r in rows)} errors')
 248      if len({(r['pid'], r['pid_start']) for r in rows}) > 1:
 249          why.append('more than one server PID')
 250      if run.get('exit_code') != 0 or run.get('killed'):
 251          why.append(f'server exit {run.get("exit_code")}, SIGKILL {run.get("killed")}')
 252      for k in ('exception', 'stopped'):
 253          if info.get(k) or run.get(k):
 254              why.append(f'{k}: {info.get(k) or run.get(k)}')
 255      if run.get('set_sha256_end') != run['set_sha256']:
 256          why.append(f'set SHA-256 at the end {run.get("set_sha256_end")} != start')
 257      return ('VALID' if not why else 'INVALID'), why
 258  
 259  
 260  def report(run, rows, info, m):
 261      s_label, why = run['validity']
 262      L = [f'MIND-B1 {run["utc"]}  code {run["code_sha256"][:12]}  set {run["set_sha256"][:12]} ({run["set_rows"]} '
 263           f'rows, {run["set_path"]})  base {run["git_head"]}',
 264           f'server: {" ".join(run["command"])}',
 265           f'n_ctx {info.get("n_ctx")}; prompt tokens (min, max) {info.get("prompt_tokens")}',
 266           f'letter ids {info.get("letter_ids")}; name ids {info.get("name_ids")}',
 267           f'ACCURACY {s_label}' + (f': {"; ".join(why)}' if why else ' (temperature 0; 160/160 rows; one server PID; '
 268                                    'exit 0; set hash unchanged)'),
 269           '* timings NOT MEASUREMENTS (agent resident allowed)']
 270      L += [f'block {x["block"]}: {x["duration_s"]} s*' + ('  OVER ~4 min (continued)' if x['over_warn'] else '')
 271            for x in info.get('blocks', [])]
 272      L += ['', 'block   strict        acceptable    copy rate      near-ties (margin < 0.05)']
 273      for k, v in m['blocks'].items():
 274          L.append(f'{k:<7} {v["strict"]:.3f} {round(v["strict"] * v["n"]):>2}/{v["n"]}  '
 275                   f'{v["acceptable"]:.3f} {round(v["acceptable"] * v["n"]):>2}/{v["n"]}  '
 276                   f'{(v["copy_rate"] or 0):.3f} {v["copy"][0]:>2}/{v["copy"][1]}  {v["near_ties"]}')
 277      L += ['', 'recall per strict label (hits/n):']
 278      L += [f'  {k:<7} ' + '  '.join(f'{a} {h}/{n}' for a, (h, n) in v['recall'].items()) for k, v in m['blocks'].items()]
 279      L += ['', 'confusion (rows strict, columns chosen):']
 280      for k, v in m['blocks'].items():
 281          L.append(f'  {k}  ' + '; '.join(f'{a} -> ' + ', '.join(f'{c} {n}' for c, n in sorted(r.items()))
 282                                           for a, r in sorted(v['confusion'].items())))
 283      L += ['', f'copied current activity (ids): ' + '; '.join(f'{k} {v["copied_ids"]}' for k, v in m['blocks'].items())]
 284      L += ['', f'PASS rule: acceptable >= {PASS["acceptable"]} and strict >= {PASS["strict"]} in both orders, '
 285                f'flips <= {PASS["max_flips"]}/40']
 286      L += [f'  arm {a}: flips {len(v["flips"])} {v["flips"]}  -> {v["verdict"]}' for a, v in m['arms'].items()]
 287      L += ['', 'id  strict (also ok)       A-FWD          A-REV          B-FWD          B-REV']
 288      by = {(r['arm'], r['order'], r['id']): r for r in rows}
 289      for it in run['items']:
 290          cells = []
 291          for arm, order in BLOCKS:
 292              r = by.get((arm, order, it['id']), {})
 293              cells.append(f'{r["top"]} {r["probs"][r["top"]]:.2f}' if 'top' in r else r.get('error', '-')[:14])
 294          lab = it['strict'] + (f' ({",".join(it["also_ok"])})' if it['also_ok'] else '')
 295          L.append(f'{it["id"]:<3} {lab:<22} ' + ''.join(f'{c:<15}' for c in cells))
 296      for arm in ('A', 'B'):
 297          if f'prompt_{arm}_item{SHOW_ITEM}' in info:
 298              L += ['', f'--- rendered prompt, arm {arm} FWD, item {SHOW_ITEM} (/detokenize) ---',
 299                    info[f'prompt_{arm}_item{SHOW_ITEM}']]
 300      if info.get('stopped'):
 301          L.append(f'STOPPED: {info["stopped"]}')
 302      if run.get('exception'):
 303          L.append(f'EXCEPTION (runner): {run["exception"]}')
 304      L.append(f'server exit code {run.get("exit_code")}; SIGKILL needed {run.get("killed")}')
 305      return '\n'.join(L) + '\n'
 306  
 307  
 308  def finish(out, run, rows, info):
 309      """Metrics, validity, run.json, report.txt and artifacts.sha256, always (also after an exception)."""
 310      run['metrics'] = metrics(rows, {'items': run['items']})
 311      run['validity'] = validity(rows, info, run)
 312      (out / 'run.json').write_text(json.dumps(run, indent=1) + '\n')
 313      text = report(run, rows, info, run['metrics'])
 314      (out / 'report.txt').write_text(text)
 315      (out / 'artifacts.sha256').write_text(''.join(f'{sha(p.read_bytes())}  {p.name}\n' for p in sorted(out.iterdir())
 316                                                    if p.name != 'artifacts.sha256'))
 317      return text
 318  
 319  
 320  def main():
 321      ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
 322      ap.add_argument('--set', default=str(SET))
 323      a = ap.parse_args()
 324      s, set_sha = load_set(a.set)
 325      b_bytes = b0.SET.read_bytes()
 326      if sha(b_bytes) != B0_SET_SHA256:
 327          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {b0.SET} SHA-256 changed')
 328      b = json.loads(b_bytes)
 329      if bad := mem1.pinned_problems()[0]:
 330          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {bad}')
 331      if not b0.repro.port_free():
 332          raise SystemExit('refused: port 8080 in use (never stops a server it did not start)')
 333      utc = time.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ', time.gmtime())
 334      out = OUT_ROOT / utc
 335      out.mkdir(parents=True)
 336      run = {'utc': utc, 'set_path': str(a.set), 'set_sha256': set_sha, 'set_rows': len(s['items']),
 337             'b0_set_sha256': B0_SET_SHA256, 'code_sha256': sha(Path(__file__).read_bytes()),
 338             'picker_sha256': sha(Path(picker.__file__).read_bytes()), 'b0_sha256': sha(Path(b0.__file__).read_bytes()),
 339             'git_head': subprocess.run(['git', '-C', str(HERE), 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], capture_output=True,
 340                                        text=True).stdout.strip(),
 341             'command': mem1.command(CTX), 'decode_b': {**b0.DECODE, **b0.PICK, 'cache_prompt': True},
 342             'missions': s['missions'], 'items': s['items'], 'timings': 'NOT MEASUREMENTS'}
 343      (out / 'run.json').write_text(json.dumps(run, indent=1) + '\n')  # set SHA-256 on disk before the server starts
 344      with open(out / 'requests.jsonl', 'x') as rq, open(out / 'server.log', 'x') as log:
 345          def emit(row):
 346              rq.write(json.dumps(row) + '\n')
 347              rq.flush()
 348          killed, rows, info, proc, exc = False, [], {}, None, None
 349          try:
 350              proc = subprocess.Popen(mem1.command(CTX), stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
 351                                      start_new_session=True)
 352              t0 = time.monotonic()
 353              while not cr.healthy():
 354                  if proc.poll() is not None or time.monotonic() - t0 > LOAD_TIMEOUT_S:
 355                      raise RuntimeError(f'llama-server not healthy (exit {proc.poll()})')
 356                  time.sleep(0.1)
 357              run['server_pid'] = proc.pid
 358              rows, info = run_blocks(Server(), s, b, proc.pid, emit)
 359          except BaseException as e:  # recorded; the report and manifest are still written, then a stop re-raises
 360              exc = e
 361              run['exception'] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 362          finally:
 363              if proc is not None and proc.poll() is None:
 364                  os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
 365                  try:
 366                      proc.wait(timeout=TERM_WAIT_S)
 367                  except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
 368                      os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
 369                      proc.wait()
 370                      killed = True
 371              run.update(exit_code=proc.returncode if proc else None, killed=killed, info=info,
 372                         set_sha256_end=sha(Path(a.set).read_bytes()))
 373      print(finish(out, run, rows, info) + f'\n{out}')
 374      if exc is not None and not isinstance(exc, Exception):
 375          raise exc
 376  
 377  
 378  if __name__ == '__main__':
 379      main()
END
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b0_cache/b0.py (344 lines)
   1  #!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/python
   2  """MIND-B0: prefix-cache mechanics of the mind/picker layout on the deployed one-slot server. Research only: no motors,
   3  camera, YOLO, M2, main.py or run_mission.py; no MTP, no projector. Counters (prompt_n, cache_n) and probabilities
   4  only; timings are recorded but are NOT MEASUREMENTS (an agent may be resident).
   5  
   6  Runtime: MEM-1's server (mem1.command(2048), mem1.PINNED SHA-256 checked before the start). Prompts:
   7  chat.build_prompt('gemma', ...) checked equal to the server's /apply-template (thinking off), tokenized by the server
   8  (add_special, parse_special) and sent as token ids. Decoding: temperature 0, seed 0, return_tokens.
   9  
  10  Layout (b0_set.json, SHA-256 recorded before the server starts): system = SYS + action catalogue + mission + memory +
  11  'Events so far:' + one line per event E1..Ek = the transcript T(k). Picker = [system T(k), user TAIL], scored on the
  12  first generated token (n_predict 1, n_probs 1000, as picker.Gemma.decide) over the five names' token ids. Chat SHARED =
  13  [system T(1), user SPEECH]; chat SEPARATE = [system COMPANION_SYS, user SPEECH]; both n_predict 32, stop <turn|>. E2 is
  14  filled at run time with the speech and R4's reply.
  15  Before any /completion: every name after the model turn must be exactly one token, all five distinct (else STOP:
  16  a finding), T(4) + tail with an empty reply must be 400..668 tokens (700 minus the 32-token reply), n_ctx 2048.
  17  
  18  Sequence: R1..R8 (cache_prompt true), then R1, R3, R5, R7, R8 again with cache_prompt false (FRESH, same ids). For each
  19  request: ids hash/count, token LCP with the slot's previous prompt + generated tokens, prompt_n, cache_n, probabilities,
  20  prompt_ms, wall, server PID + start time. Criteria (criteria()): C1 R2/R3/R5/R8 cache_n >= min(LCP, n - 1) (the server
  21  evaluates at least one prompt token); C2 R5 cache_n >= tokens of T(1) without the tail (BOS + system turn opening +
  22  T(1) text); C3 R7 prompt_n, informational; C4 cached vs fresh: same top action for all five, max |dp| (renormalised
  23  over the five) <= 0.02; C5 one server PID, 0 request errors, no exception, all 13 requests, server exit code 0
  24  after SIGTERM (no SIGKILL). An exception keeps the rows already collected, is recorded in run.json and the report,
  25  fails the criteria it affects, and run.json, report.txt and artifacts.sha256 are still written (finish()).
  26  
  27  Usage (from proot Debian or native Termux, with Termux python): b0.py
  28  Writes ~/robot_runs/mind_b0/<UTC>/: run.json, requests.jsonl, server.log, report.txt, artifacts.sha256.
  29  """
  30  import hashlib
  31  import json
  32  import math
  33  import os
  34  import signal
  35  import subprocess
  36  import sys
  37  import time
  38  from pathlib import Path
  39  
  40  sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
  41  HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
  42  sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE.parents[1] / 'memory_context'))
  43  import mem1  # noqa: E402  command(), PINNED, pinned_problems(), lcp(); imports chat and the harness
  44  
  45  cr, chat, repro = mem1.cr, mem1.chat, mem1.repro
  46  SET = HERE / 'b0_set.json'
  47  OUT_ROOT = cr.HOME / 'robot_runs' / 'mind_b0'
  48  CTX = 2048
  49  DECODE = {'temperature': 0, 'seed': 0, 'stream': False, 'return_tokens': True}
  50  PICK = {'n_predict': 1, 'n_probs': 1000, 'post_sampling_probs': False}  # picker.Gemma.decide
  51  CHAT = {'n_predict': 32, 'stop': chat.get_stop_tokens('gemma')}
  52  SIZE = (400, 700 - CHAT['n_predict'])
  53  MAX_DP = 0.02
  54  LOAD_TIMEOUT_S, REQUEST_TIMEOUT_S, TERM_WAIT_S = 120, 300, 30
  55  # (id, kind, k): picker at T(k); 'shared' chat at T(1); 'separate' chat
  56  SEQUENCE = [('R1', 'picker', 0), ('R2', 'picker', 0), ('R3', 'picker', 1), ('R4', 'shared', 1),
  57              ('R5', 'picker', 2), ('R6', 'separate', None), ('R7', 'picker', 3), ('R8', 'picker', 4)]
  58  FRESH = ('R1', 'R3', 'R5', 'R7', 'R8')
  59  
  60  
  61  def sha(b):
  62      return hashlib.sha256(b if isinstance(b, bytes) else b.encode()).hexdigest()
  63  
  64  
  65  def transcript(s, k, reply=''):
  66      """T(k): the system text with events E1..Ek (E2 = the speech and R4's reply)."""
  67      e2 = s['e2_format'].format(speech=s['speech'], reply=' '.join(reply.split()))
  68      events = [e.format(e2=e2) for e in s['events'][:k]]
  69      return '\n'.join([s['sys'], '', 'Actions:', *s['catalogue'], '', f'Mission: {s["mission"]}', 'Memory:',
  70                        *(f'- {m}' for m in s['memory']), '', 'Events so far:', *(f'- {e}' for e in events)])
  71  
  72  
  73  def messages(s, kind, k, reply=''):
  74      if kind == 'picker':
  75          return [{'role': 'system', 'content': transcript(s, k, reply)}, {'role': 'user', 'content': s['tail']}]
  76      if kind == 'shared':
  77          return [{'role': 'system', 'content': transcript(s, k)}, {'role': 'user', 'content': s['speech']}]
  78      return [{'role': 'system', 'content': s['companion_sys']}, {'role': 'user', 'content': s['speech']}]
  79  
  80  
  81  def tokenize(post, text, special=True):
  82      return post('/tokenize', {'content': text, 'add_special': special, 'parse_special': True})['tokens']
  83  
  84  
  85  def render(post, msgs):
  86      """(prompt text, ids, problem): chat.build_prompt checked against the server's GGUF template."""
  87      p = chat.build_prompt('gemma', msgs)
  88      t = post('/apply-template', {'messages': msgs, 'chat_template_kwargs': {'enable_thinking': False}})
  89      return p, tokenize(post, p), None if t.get('prompt') == p else 'GGUF template render differs from build_prompt'
  90  
  91  
  92  def name_ids(post, prompt, ids, names):
  93      """({name: ids after the prompt}, problems): each name must add exactly one token to the prompt's ids, the five
  94      distinct. A problem STOPs the run before any inference."""
  95      tails, why = {}, []
  96      for n in names:
  97          full = tokenize(post, prompt + n)
  98          tails[n] = full[len(ids):]
  99          if full[:len(ids)] != ids:
 100              why.append(f'{n!r}: prompt ids change when the name follows')
 101          elif len(tails[n]) != 1:
 102              why.append(f'{n!r}: {len(tails[n])} tokens after the model turn {tails[n]}, not one')
 103      firsts = [t[0] for t in tails.values() if t]
 104      if len(set(firsts)) != len(firsts):
 105          why.append(f'first tokens not distinct: {tails}')
 106      return tails, why
 107  
 108  
 109  def probs(res, ids_by_name):
 110      """Raw probabilities of the names' tokens at the first generated position (picker.Gemma.decide), None outside
 111      the top n_probs; renormalised over the five only when all are present."""
 112      by_id = {e['id']: (e['prob'] if 'prob' in e else math.exp(e['logprob']))
 113               for e in res['completion_probabilities'][0]['top_logprobs']}
 114      raw = {n: by_id.get(i[0]) for n, i in ids_by_name.items()}
 115      mass = sum(v for v in raw.values() if v is not None)
 116      norm = {n: v / mass for n, v in raw.items()} if None not in raw.values() and mass > 0 else None
 117      return raw, norm
 118  
 119  
 120  def pid_start(pid):
 121      try:
 122          return Path(f'/proc/{pid}/stat').read_text().rsplit(')', 1)[1].split()[19]  # field 22: start time
 123      except (OSError, IndexError):
 124          return None
 125  
 126  
 127  def run_sequence(post, s, pid, emit):
 128      """Checks, then R1..R8 and the FRESH repeats, in order; returns (rows, info). Stops at the first problem or error;
 129      an exception is recorded in info['exception'] and the rows collected so far are kept (the criteria then FAIL)."""
 130      rows, info = [], {'problems': []}
 131      try:
 132          sequence(post, s, pid, emit, rows, info)
 133      except Exception as e:
 134          info['exception'] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 135          info['stopped'] = f'exception after {len(rows)} requests: {info["exception"]}'
 136      return rows, info
 137  
 138  
 139  def sequence(post, s, pid, emit, rows, info):
 140      msgs0 = messages(s, 'picker', 0)
 141      p0, ids0, bad = render(post, msgs0)
 142      info['name_ids'], why = name_ids(post, p0, ids0, s['names'])
 143      info['problems'] += why + ([bad] if bad else [])
 144      _, ids4, _ = render(post, messages(s, 'picker', 4))
 145      info['t4_tail_tokens_empty_reply'] = len(ids4)
 146      if not SIZE[0] <= len(ids4) <= SIZE[1]:
 147          info['problems'].append(f'T(4) + tail = {len(ids4)} tokens with an empty reply, not in {SIZE}')
 148      props = post('/props', None)
 149      info['n_ctx'] = props.get('default_generation_settings', {}).get('n_ctx')
 150      if info['n_ctx'] != CTX:
 151          info['problems'].append(f'server n_ctx {info["n_ctx"]!r} != {CTX}')
 152      info['parts'] = {'sys+catalogue': len(tokenize(post, '\n'.join([s['sys'], *s['catalogue']]), False)),
 153                       'mission+memory': len(tokenize(post, '\n'.join([s['mission'], *s['memory']]), False)),
 154                       'tail': len(tokenize(post, s['tail'], False)), 'speech': len(tokenize(post, s['speech'], False)),
 155                       'companion_sys': len(tokenize(post, s['companion_sys'], False))}
 156      # C2's reference: BOS + '<|turn>system\n' + T(1), i.e. the R4 prompt up to the end of E1
 157      info['t1_prefix_tokens'] = len(tokenize(post, '<|turn>system\n' + transcript(s, 1)))
 158      if info['problems']:
 159          info['stopped'] = 'STOP before any inference: ' + '; '.join(info['problems'])
 160          return
 161      slot, reply, sent = None, '', {}
 162      plan = [(r, kind, k, True) for r, kind, k in SEQUENCE] + [(r, None, None, False) for r in FRESH]
 163      for rid, kind, k, cache in plan:
 164          if cache:
 165              prompt, ids, bad = render(post, messages(s, kind, k, reply))
 166              if bad:
 167                  info['stopped'] = f'{rid}: {bad}'
 168                  break
 169              sent[rid] = (kind, k, ids)
 170          else:
 171              kind, k, ids = sent[rid]
 172          body = {'prompt': ids, **DECODE, **(PICK if kind == 'picker' else CHAT), 'cache_prompt': cache}
 173          row = {'id': rid if cache else rid + '_FRESH', 'kind': kind, 'k': k, 'cache_prompt': cache,
 174                 'prompt_tokens': len(ids), 'prompt_ids_sha256': sha(json.dumps(ids)),
 175                 'lcp_prev_slot': mem1.lcp(ids, slot) if slot is not None else None,
 176                 'pid': pid, 'pid_start': pid_start(pid)}
 177          if kind == 'picker' and not cache:
 178              row['probs_cached_ref'] = rid
 179          t0 = time.perf_counter()
 180          try:
 181              res = post('/completion', body)
 182          except Exception as e:  # fail closed: recorded, the sequence ends
 183              row.update(error=f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}', wall_s=round(time.perf_counter() - t0, 3))
 184              rows.append(row)
 185              emit(row)
 186              info['stopped'] = f'{row["id"]} failed: {e!r}'
 187              break
 188          tm, gen = res.get('timings', {}), res.get('tokens') or []
 189          row.update(wall_s=round(time.perf_counter() - t0, 3), prompt_n=tm.get('prompt_n'), cache_n=tm.get('cache_n'),
 190                     prompt_ms=tm.get('prompt_ms'), generated_ids=gen, content=res.get('content'),
 191                     tokens_cached=res.get('tokens_cached'), timings=tm)
 192          if kind == 'picker':
 193              row['probs_raw'], row['probs'] = probs(res, info['name_ids'])
 194              row['top'] = max(row['probs'], key=row['probs'].get) if row['probs'] else None
 195          if rid == 'R4':
 196              reply = res.get('content') or ''
 197              info['reply'] = reply
 198          slot = ids + gen
 199          rows.append(row)
 200          emit(row)
 201  
 202  
 203  def criteria(rows, info, exit_code, killed, exception=None):
 204      """[(name, PASS|FAIL|INFO, numbers)], from the recorded rows alone; any exception (run_sequence's or the
 205      runner's) fails C5, missing rows fail C1, C2 and C4."""
 206      r = {x['id']: x for x in rows}
 207      out = []
 208  
 209      def got(rid, key):
 210          return r.get(rid, {}).get(key)
 211      c1 = []
 212      for rid in ('R2', 'R3', 'R5', 'R8'):
 213          n, lcp, cn = got(rid, 'prompt_tokens'), got(rid, 'lcp_prev_slot'), got(rid, 'cache_n')
 214          need = min(lcp, n - 1) if None not in (n, lcp) else None
 215          c1.append((rid, cn, need, cn is not None and need is not None and cn >= need))
 216      out.append(('C1', 'PASS' if all(x[3] for x in c1) else 'FAIL',
 217                  '; '.join(f'{a}: cache_n {b} vs shared {c}' for a, b, c, _ in c1)))
 218      cn, need = got('R5', 'cache_n'), info.get('t1_prefix_tokens')
 219      out.append(('C2', 'PASS' if None not in (cn, need) and cn >= need else 'FAIL',
 220                  f'R5 cache_n {cn} vs T(1) without tail {need}'))
 221      out.append(('C3', 'INFO', f'R7 prompt_n {got("R7", "prompt_n")} (rebuild cost after the separate chat; '
 222                  f'cache_n {got("R7", "cache_n")}, prompt {got("R7", "prompt_tokens")})'))
 223      c4, dps = [], []
 224      for rid in FRESH:
 225          a, b = got(rid, 'probs'), got(rid + '_FRESH', 'probs')
 226          if a is None or b is None:
 227              c4.append((rid, None, None, False))
 228              continue
 229          dp = max(abs(a[n] - b[n]) for n in a)
 230          dps.append(dp)
 231          c4.append((rid, got(rid, 'top'), got(rid + '_FRESH', 'top'), got(rid, 'top') == got(rid + '_FRESH', 'top')))
 232      ok4 = all(x[3] for x in c4) and len(dps) == len(FRESH) and max(dps) <= MAX_DP
 233      out.append(('C4', 'PASS' if ok4 else 'FAIL', (f'max |dp| {max(dps):.6f}; ' if dps else 'no pairs; ') +
 234                  '; '.join(f'{a}: cached {b} / fresh {c}' for a, b, c, _ in c4)))
 235      pids = {(x['pid'], x['pid_start']) for x in rows}
 236      errors = sum('error' in x for x in rows)
 237      exc = exception or info.get('exception')
 238      ok5 = len(pids) == 1 and errors == 0 and exit_code == 0 and not killed and not exc and \
 239          len(rows) == len(SEQUENCE) + len(FRESH)
 240      out.append(('C5', 'PASS' if ok5 else 'FAIL', f'PIDs {sorted(pids)}; {len(rows)} requests, {errors} errors; '
 241                  f'exit code {exit_code}, SIGKILL {killed}; exception {exc}'))
 242      return out
 243  
 244  
 245  def report(run, rows, info, crit):
 246      L = [f'MIND-B0 {run["utc"]}  code {run["code_sha256"][:12]}  set {run["set_sha256"][:12]}  base {run["git_head"]}',
 247           f'server: {" ".join(run["command"])}', f'--slot-save-path listed in --help: {run["slot_save_path_listed"]}',
 248           f'n_ctx {info.get("n_ctx")}; T(4)+tail (empty reply) {info.get("t4_tail_tokens_empty_reply")} tokens; '
 249           f'T(1) without tail {info.get("t1_prefix_tokens")} tokens; parts {info.get("parts")}',
 250           f'name ids {info.get("name_ids")}', f'R4 reply: {info.get("reply")!r}']
 251      if info.get('stopped'):
 252          L.append(f'STOPPED: {info["stopped"]}')
 253      if run.get('exception'):
 254          L.append(f'EXCEPTION (runner): {run["exception"]}')
 255      L += ['', 'id        kind      cache  n    LCP  prompt_n cache_n  top      p(top)  prompt_ms* wall_s*']
 256      for x in rows:
 257          p = x.get('probs') or {}
 258          L.append(f'{x["id"]:<9} {x["kind"]:<9} {str(x["cache_prompt"])[0]:<6} {x["prompt_tokens"]:<4} '
 259                   f'{str(x["lcp_prev_slot"]):<4} {str(x.get("prompt_n")):<8} {str(x.get("cache_n")):<8} '
 260                   f'{str(x.get("top")):<8} {p.get(x.get("top"), float("nan")):.4f}  '
 261                   f'{str(x.get("prompt_ms")):<10.10} {x.get("wall_s")}' + (f'  ERROR {x["error"]}' if 'error' in x
 262                                                                             else ''))
 263      L += ['* timings NOT MEASUREMENTS (agent resident allowed; no thermal or core control)', '',
 264            'probabilities (renormalised over the five; raw mass in brackets):']
 265      for x in rows:
 266          if x.get('probs_raw'):
 267              mass = sum(v for v in x['probs_raw'].values() if v is not None)
 268              L.append(f'  {x["id"]:<9} ' + ' '.join(f'{n}={v:.4f}' for n, v in (x['probs'] or {}).items()) +
 269                       f'  [{mass:.4f}]')
 270      L += ['', 'criteria:'] + [f'  {c}  {v:<4}  {t}' for c, v, t in crit]
 271      L.append(f'server exit code {run.get("exit_code")}; SIGKILL needed {run.get("killed")}')
 272      return '\n'.join(L) + '\n'
 273  
 274  
 275  def main():
 276      s_bytes = SET.read_bytes()
 277      s = json.loads(s_bytes)
 278      if bad := mem1.pinned_problems()[0]:
 279          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {bad}')
 280      if not repro.port_free():
 281          raise SystemExit('refused: port 8080 in use (never stops a server it did not start)')
 282      utc = time.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ', time.gmtime())
 283      out = OUT_ROOT / utc
 284      out.mkdir(parents=True)
 285      help_text = subprocess.run([mem1.SERVER, '--help'], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60).stdout
 286      run = {'utc': utc, 'set_sha256': sha(s_bytes), 'code_sha256': sha(Path(__file__).read_bytes()),
 287             'mem1_sha256': sha(Path(mem1.__file__).read_bytes()), 'pinned_sha256': mem1.PINNED,
 288             'git_head': subprocess.run(['git', '-C', str(HERE), 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], capture_output=True,
 289                                        text=True).stdout.strip(),
 290             'command': mem1.command(CTX), 'decode': DECODE, 'picker': PICK, 'chat': CHAT,
 291             'slot_save_path_listed': '--slot-save-path' in help_text, 'timings': 'NOT MEASUREMENTS'}
 292      (out / 'run.json').write_text(json.dumps(run, indent=1) + '\n')  # set SHA-256 on disk before the server starts
 293  
 294      def post(path, body):
 295          if body is None:
 296              return mem1.props_json()
 297          return cr.post(path, body, timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT_S)
 298  
 299      with open(out / 'requests.jsonl', 'x') as rq, open(out / 'server.log', 'x') as log:
 300          def emit(row):
 301              rq.write(json.dumps(row) + '\n')
 302              rq.flush()
 303          killed, rows, info, proc, exc = False, [], {}, None, None
 304          try:
 305              proc = subprocess.Popen(mem1.command(CTX), stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
 306                                      start_new_session=True)
 307              t0 = time.monotonic()
 308              while not cr.healthy():
 309                  if proc.poll() is not None or time.monotonic() - t0 > LOAD_TIMEOUT_S:
 310                      raise RuntimeError(f'llama-server not healthy (exit {proc.poll()})')
 311                  time.sleep(0.1)
 312              run['server_pid'], run['server_comm'] = proc.pid, Path(f'/proc/{proc.pid}/comm').read_text().strip()
 313              rows, info = run_sequence(post, s, proc.pid, emit)
 314          except BaseException as e:  # recorded; the report and manifest are still written, then a stop re-raises
 315              exc = e
 316              run['exception'] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 317          finally:
 318              if proc is not None and proc.poll() is None:
 319                  os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
 320                  try:
 321                      proc.wait(timeout=TERM_WAIT_S)
 322                  except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
 323                      os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
 324                      proc.wait()
 325                      killed = True
 326              run.update(exit_code=proc.returncode if proc else None, killed=killed, info=info)
 327      print(finish(out, run, rows, info) + f'\n{out}')
 328      if exc is not None and not isinstance(exc, Exception):
 329          raise exc
 330  
 331  
 332  def finish(out, run, rows, info):
 333      """Criteria, run.json, report.txt and artifacts.sha256, always (also after an exception); returns the report."""
 334      run['criteria'] = criteria(rows, info, run.get('exit_code'), run.get('killed'), run.get('exception'))
 335      (out / 'run.json').write_text(json.dumps(run, indent=1) + '\n')
 336      text = report(run, rows, info, run['criteria'])
 337      (out / 'report.txt').write_text(text)
 338      (out / 'artifacts.sha256').write_text(''.join(f'{sha(p.read_bytes())}  {p.name}\n' for p in sorted(out.iterdir())
 339                                                    if p.name != 'artifacts.sha256'))
 340      return text
 341  
 342  
 343  if __name__ == '__main__':
 344      main()
END
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b0_cache/test_b0.py (126 lines)
   1  """Offline tests for b0.py with a fake server: LCP math, criteria, the token-uniqueness stop. No server, no phone."""
   2  import json
   3  import re
   4  import sys
   5  import tempfile
   6  import unittest
   7  from pathlib import Path
   8  
   9  sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
  10  sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
  11  import b0  # noqa: E402
  12  
  13  S = json.loads(b0.SET.read_text())
  14  
  15  
  16  class Fake:
  17      """Word-level tokenizer; /completion follows llama-server's rule cache_n = min(LCP, n - 1) when cache_prompt."""
  18  
  19      def __init__(self, split=(), cache=True, fresh_shift=0.0, no_probs_at=None):
  20          self.vocab, self.slot, self.split, self.cache, self.shift, self.completions = {}, [], split, cache, \
  21              fresh_shift, 0
  22          self.no_probs_at = no_probs_at  # this completion (1-based) comes without completion_probabilities
  23  
  24      def ids(self, text):
  25          out = []
  26          for w in re.findall(r'<\|turn>|<turn\|>|\w+|\s|[^\w\s]', text):
  27              for piece in ([w[:2], w[2:]] if w in self.split else [w]):
  28                  out.append(self.vocab.setdefault(piece, len(self.vocab) + 10))
  29          return out
  30  
  31      def __call__(self, path, body):
  32          if path == '/tokenize':
  33              return {'tokens': ([2] if body['add_special'] else []) + self.ids(body['content'])}
  34          if path == '/apply-template':
  35              return {'prompt': b0.chat.build_prompt('gemma', body['messages'])}
  36          if path == '/props':
  37              return {'default_generation_settings': {'n_ctx': 2048}}
  38          assert path == '/completion'
  39          self.completions += 1
  40          ids = body['prompt']
  41          cn = min(b0.mem1.lcp(ids, self.slot), len(ids) - 1) if body['cache_prompt'] and self.cache else 0
  42          gen = [self.vocab.setdefault(w, len(self.vocab) + 10) for w in (['look'] if body['n_predict'] == 1 else
  43                                                                            ['I', 'am', 'exploring'])]
  44          self.slot = ids + gen
  45          shift = 0 if body['cache_prompt'] else self.shift
  46          p = {'explore': 0.1, 'visit': 0.2, 'greet': 0.3 - shift, 'look': 0.35 + shift, 'wait': 0.05}
  47          if self.completions == self.no_probs_at:
  48              return {'content': 'look', 'tokens': gen, 'timings': {'prompt_n': len(ids) - cn, 'cache_n': cn}}
  49          return {'content': 'I am exploring', 'tokens': gen,
  50                  'timings': {'prompt_n': len(ids) - cn, 'cache_n': cn, 'prompt_ms': 1.0},
  51                  'completion_probabilities': [{'top_logprobs': [{'id': self.vocab[n], 'prob': v}
  52                                                                 for n, v in p.items()]}]}
  53  
  54  
  55  def run(fake):
  56      b0.SIZE = (1, 10 ** 6)  # the fake tokenizer is word-level
  57      return b0.run_sequence(fake, S, 123, lambda row: None)
  58  
  59  
  60  class T(unittest.TestCase):
  61      def test_lcp(self):
  62          self.assertEqual(b0.mem1.lcp([1, 2, 3], [1, 2, 4, 5]), 2)
  63          self.assertEqual(b0.mem1.lcp([1, 2], [1, 2, 3]), 2)
  64  
  65      def test_full_sequence_passes(self):
  66          rows, info = run(Fake())
  67          self.assertNotIn('stopped', info)
  68          self.assertEqual([r['id'] for r in rows], [x[0] for x in b0.SEQUENCE] + [r + '_FRESH' for r in b0.FRESH])
  69          r = {x['id']: x for x in rows}
  70          self.assertEqual(r['R2']['cache_n'], r['R2']['prompt_tokens'] - 1)  # repeat: n - 1
  71          self.assertEqual(r['R2']['lcp_prev_slot'], r['R2']['prompt_tokens'])
  72          self.assertIn('I am exploring', b0.transcript(S, 2, info['reply']))
  73          self.assertEqual(r['R5_FRESH']['prompt_ids_sha256'], r['R5']['prompt_ids_sha256'])
  74          self.assertLess(r['R7']['cache_n'], 10)  # after the separate chat only BOS, turn opening, 'You are ' survive
  75          crit = dict((c, v) for c, v, _ in b0.criteria(rows, info, 0, False))
  76          self.assertEqual(crit, {'C1': 'PASS', 'C2': 'PASS', 'C3': 'INFO', 'C4': 'PASS', 'C5': 'PASS'})
  77  
  78      def test_criteria_fail(self):
  79          rows, info = run(Fake(cache=False, fresh_shift=0.1))
  80          crit = dict((c, v) for c, v, _ in b0.criteria(rows, info, 0, False))
  81          self.assertEqual((crit['C1'], crit['C2'], crit['C4']), ('FAIL', 'FAIL', 'FAIL'))
  82          rows, info = run(Fake(fresh_shift=0.03))  # same top, |dp| 0.03 > 0.02
  83          self.assertEqual(dict((c, v) for c, v, _ in b0.criteria(rows, info, 0, False))['C4'], 'FAIL')
  84          for code, killed in ((1, False), (-15, False), (0, True)):
  85              self.assertEqual(b0.criteria(rows, info, code, killed)[4][1], 'FAIL')
  86          self.assertEqual(b0.criteria(rows[:-1], info, 0, False)[4][1], 'FAIL')  # a request missing
  87  
  88      def test_exception_keeps_rows_and_writes_report(self):
  89          rows, info = run(Fake(no_probs_at=5))  # R5's reply lacks completion_probabilities
  90          self.assertEqual([r['id'] for r in rows], ['R1', 'R2', 'R3', 'R4'])
  91          self.assertIn("KeyError: 'completion_probabilities'", info['exception'])
  92          run_rec = {'utc': 'T', 'code_sha256': 'c' * 64, 'set_sha256': 's' * 64, 'git_head': 'h', 'command': ['x'],
  93                     'slot_save_path_listed': True, 'exit_code': 0, 'killed': False, 'info': info}
  94          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
  95              out = Path(d)
  96              (out / 'requests.jsonl').write_text('')
  97              text = b0.finish(out, run_rec, rows, info)
  98              saved = json.loads((out / 'run.json').read_text())
  99              self.assertIn('completion_probabilities', saved['info']['exception'])
 100              self.assertEqual({c: v for c, v, _ in saved['criteria']},
 101                               {'C1': 'FAIL', 'C2': 'FAIL', 'C3': 'INFO', 'C4': 'FAIL', 'C5': 'FAIL'})
 102              self.assertEqual((out / 'report.txt').read_text(), text)
 103              self.assertIn('STOPPED: exception after 4 requests', text)
 104              names = [l.split()[1] for l in (out / 'artifacts.sha256').read_text().splitlines()]
 105              self.assertEqual(names, ['report.txt', 'requests.jsonl', 'run.json'])
 106          crit = b0.criteria(*run(Fake()), 0, False, exception='RuntimeError: x')  # a runner exception alone
 107          self.assertEqual(crit[4][1], 'FAIL')
 108  
 109      def test_name_token_stop(self):
 110          fake = Fake(split=('greet',))
 111          rows, info = run(fake)
 112          self.assertEqual((rows, fake.completions), ([], 0))
 113          self.assertIn('STOP before any inference', info['stopped'])
 114          self.assertIn("'greet': 2 tokens", info['stopped'])
 115  
 116      def test_name_duplicate_stop(self):
 117          fake = Fake()
 118          fake.ids('look')  # fix 'look' first, then give 'wait' the same id
 119          fake.vocab['wait'] = fake.vocab['look']
 120          rows, info = run(fake)
 121          self.assertEqual((rows, fake.completions), ([], 0))
 122          self.assertIn('not distinct', info['stopped'])
 123  
 124  
 125  if __name__ == '__main__':
 126      unittest.main()
END

BEGIN TEST item 3 rendered for V3 FWD (offline: b2.messages + the template layout seen in report.txt)
<bos><|turn>system
You are the mind of a small home robot. A separate safety program drives the wheels, avoids obstacles and may refuse any action. Each time something happens, you choose the robot's next action, using the mission, what just happened and the memory notes.

Actions:
explore: drive on and explore the home
approach: drive to a person
greet: stop and say hello or goodbye to a person
look: stop and look around in place
wait: stay still and wait

Rules (the first rule that fits decides):
1. Someone asks the robot to stop, stay, wait, hold on or pause: wait.
2. Someone asks the robot to come, come here or follow them: approach.
3. Someone says hello or goodbye to the robot, or waves at it: greet.
4. Someone asks the robot to look at or check something: look.
5. Follow memory notes about people (for example: leave them alone, or greet them).
6. Quiet mission: do not approach or greet a person who has not invited the robot; explore.
7. A person is busy, does not want the robot, or tells it to carry on or go: explore.
8. A person is talking with the robot: wait.
9. The mission or task is finished: wait.
10. The person the robot was approaching is gone: look.
11. The robot entered a new room: look. A look-around is finished: explore.
12. Mission about people: a far or medium person: approach; a close person not yet greeted: greet.
13. Otherwise: keep the current activity.

Examples:
Mission: Explore and map the flat. | Current activity: exploring | You entered a new room: the laundry room. -> look
Mission: Find people and keep them company. | Current activity: exploring | Someone said: "Wait for me here." -> wait
Mission: Explore and map the flat. | Current activity: waiting | Someone said: "Follow me to the hallway." -> approach
Mission: Find people and keep them company. | Current activity: looking | A person appeared far away in the hallway. -> approach
Mission: Find people and keep them company. | Current activity: waiting | A person walked up to you and is now close. -> greet
Mission: Map the flat quietly and do not disturb anyone. | Current activity: exploring | A person is sitting close, typing on a laptop. -> explore
Mission: Map the flat quietly and do not disturb anyone. | Current activity: exploring | A person at medium distance said: "Hey robot, come here!" -> approach
Mission: Find people and keep them company. | Current activity: approaching | A person close said: "Sorry, I have no time now." -> explore
Mission: Help the owner find his keys. | Current activity: looking | The owner said: "Never mind, I found them." -> wait
Mission: Explore and map the flat. | Current activity: exploring | Someone said: "What's under the table?" -> look
Mission: Explore and map the flat. | Current activity: exploring | A person close said: "See you later, robot!" -> greet
Mission: Find people and keep them company. | Current activity: looking | You finished looking around the garage. -> explore
Mission: Explore and map the flat. | Current activity: exploring | Nothing new happened. -> explore<turn|>
<|turn>user
Mission: Map the flat quietly and do not disturb anyone.
Current activity: exploring
What just happened: A person is far away, reading.
Memory: none
Allowed actions: explore, approach, greet, look, wait

Which action next? First write one short sentence naming the rule that fits. Then write a new line starting with 'Action:' and one word: explore, approach, greet, look or wait.<turn|>
<|turn>model
END

<bos><|turn>system
You are the mind of a small home robot. A separate safety program drives the wheels, avoids obstacles and may refuse any action. Each time something happens, you choose the robot's next action, using the mission, what just happened and the memory notes.

Actions:
explore: drive on and explore the home
approach: drive to a person
greet: stop and say hello or goodbye to a person
look: stop and look around in place
wait: stay still and wait

Rules (the first rule that fits decides):
1. Someone asks the robot to stop, stay, wait, hold on or pause: wait.
2. Someone asks the robot to come, come here or follow them: approach.
3. Someone says hello or goodbye to the robot, or waves at it: greet.
4. Someone asks the robot to look at or check something: look.
5. Follow memory notes about people (for example: leave them alone, or greet them).
6. Quiet mission: do not approach or greet a person who has not invited the robot; explore.
7. A person is busy, does not want the robot, or tells it to carry on or go: explore.
8. A person is talking with the robot: wait.
9. The mission or task is finished: wait.
10. The person the robot was approaching is gone: look.
11. The robot entered a new room: look. A look-around is finished: explore.
12. Mission about people: a far or medium person: approach; a close person not yet greeted: greet.
13. Otherwise: keep the current activity.

Examples:
Mission: Explore and map the flat. | Current activity: exploring | You entered a new room: the laundry room. -> look
Mission: Find people and keep them company. | Current activity: exploring | Someone said: "Wait for me here." -> wait
Mission: Explore and map the flat. | Current activity: waiting | Someone said: "Follow me to the hallway." -> approach
Mission: Find people and keep them company. | Current activity: looking | A person appeared far away in the hallway. -> approach
Mission: Find people and keep them company. | Current activity: waiting | A person walked up to you and is now close. -> greet
Mission: Map the flat quietly and do not disturb anyone. | Current activity: exploring | A person is sitting close, typing on a laptop. -> explore
Mission: Map the flat quietly and do not disturb anyone. | Current activity: exploring | A person at medium distance said: "Hey robot, come here!" -> approach
Mission: Find people and keep them company. | Current activity: approaching | A person close said: "Sorry, I have no time now." -> explore
Mission: Help the owner find his keys. | Current activity: looking | The owner said: "Never mind, I found them." -> wait
Mission: Explore and map the flat. | Current activity: exploring | Someone said: "What's under the table?" -> look
Mission: Explore and map the flat. | Current activity: exploring | A person close said: "See you later, robot!" -> greet
Mission: Find people and keep them company. | Current activity: looking | You finished looking around the garage. -> explore
Mission: Explore and map the flat. | Current activity: exploring | Nothing new happened. -> explore<turn|>
<|turn>user

Current activity: exploring
What just happened: A person appeared close and said nothing.
Memory: none
Allowed actions: explore, approach, greet, look, wait

Which action next? First write one short sentence naming the rule that fits. Then write a new line starting with 'Action:' and one word: explore, approach, greet, look or wait.<turn|>
